Model architecture: 混合刚柔结构：膜式气动执行器 + 3D打印双棘轮-棘爪机构（offset配置）。非深度学习模型，无神经网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是机械结构的轻量化/节能设计（自锁机制减少能耗），而非深度学习模型的轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存等信息。能耗指标为42.6 J/抓取周期（机械能耗），非计算能耗。

Datasets and metrics: 实验指标：最大负载4200 g、力分布差异比1.75-35.29%、峰值接触力、能耗42.6 J vs 85.28 J。无标准数据集，为实物实验。

Experiments: 对比对象：传统软体抓手（负载45-210 g）和刚性抓手（力分布差异56.77-66.44%）。消融实验：摘要未说明。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关，无法提供任何模型轻量化或目标检测方面的信息。属于机械工程/农业机器人领域。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于机械工程领域，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或深度学习模型压缩，无法为"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题提供任何可借鉴的技术思路。

Abstract: Conventional robotic grippers face a significant challenge in agricultural automation: the trade-off between compliant, adaptive grasping, pressure balancing among all joints, and high load capacity, often at the cost of high energy consumption. This paper presents a novel hybrid rigid-soft gripper that integrated low-cost, membrane-based pneumatic actuators with 3D-printed dual ratchet-pawl mechanisms to simultaneously achieve shape adaptation, uniform force distribution, and energy-free self-locking. The dual-ratchet structure assembled in an offset configuration significantly increased the angular resolution of the joint locking mechanism. Key experimental results demonstrated the gripper's superior performance: a remarkable maximum load capacity of 4200 g, far exceeding that of conventional soft grippers (45-210 g); more uniform force distribution across object sizes (1.75-35.29% difference ratio) compared to a rigid gripper (56.77-66.44%), with peak contact forces remaining below surface damage thresholds; and a 50.05% reduction in total energy consumption to 42.6 J per grasp cycle, achieved by eliminating the need for continuous pneumatic pressure through the self-locking mechanism, compared to 85.28 J for a conventional soft gripper. The combination of additive manufacturing for ratchets and commercially available materials for pneumatic chambers ensured a low-cost and easily fabricated design. These findings validated that the proposed gripper successfully bridged the gap between soft compliance and rigid reliability, offering a robust and efficient solution for scalable agricultural harvesting and manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [122] [Curvature-Constrained and Constant-Speed Distributed Simultaneous Arrival Control for Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14781)
*Zhouru Xiao,Yang Lu,Weijia Yao,Min Liu,Yaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于最大一致性协议和Dubins路径几何特性的分布式切换控制方法，解决多固定翼无人机在曲率约束和恒速条件下的同时到达问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是多移动机器人（固定翼无人机）的同时到达控制问题，属于多智能体协同控制/路径规划领域，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多机器人同时到达控制；应用场景：协同包围、灾害救援、环境监测；传感器类型：未说明；目标类别：未说明。不涉及遥感图像或目标检测。

Model architecture: 不涉及深度学习模型。提出的是基于最大一致性协议的分布式切换控制律，结合最优控制与饱和比例控制。

Lightweight method: 不涉及模型轻量化。论文提到"scalable and real-time, with low communication overhead"，但这是针对控制算法的通信/计算开销，与神经网络剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署指标。仅提及"low communication overhead"和"real-time"，但这是控制层面的实时性，非深度学习推理部署。

Datasets and metrics: 通过仿真和实验验证，未使用标准遥感/目标检测数据集。评价指标为同时到达的达成情况与到达时间最优性。

Experiments: 进行了仿真和实物实验验证方法的有效性和鲁棒性，但未与目标检测方法对比。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无可借鉴的模型压缩或部署信息。摘要未说明在遥感图像上的任何应用。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于多机器人协同控制领域，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型轻量化技术。

Abstract: The simultaneous arrival of multiple mobile robots at a target point is crucial for cooperation tasks such as cooperative encirclement, disaster relief, and environmental monitoring. Although the simultaneous arrival problem itself is already complex, the problem becomes more challenging when there are constraints on the robot trajectory curvatures and the speeds are required to be constant (possibly different for different robots), and the control law for robots needs to be distributed. These constraints are typical for a multi-robot system consisting of, e.g., fixed-wing UAVs. To address this challenge, this paper proposes a distributed switching control method based on the maximum consensus protocol. By exploiting the geometric properties of Dubins paths along with optimization principles, a virtual time variable is introduced, and a hybrid control law that combines optimal control with saturated proportional control is designed. Under the proposed control law, each robot is driven to approach the maximum virtual time among its neighbors, thereby achieving simultaneous arrival under some mild conditions. Furthermore, we prove that in certain cases the proposed method attains a theoretically optimal arrival time. The approach is scalable and real-time, with low communication overhead. Its effectiveness and robustness are validated through extensive simulations and experiments.

</details>


### [123] [Interventional Causal Circuits for Safe Robot Action Testing and Failure Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14826)
*Naren Vasantakumaar,Tom Schierenbeck,Michael Beetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于因果推理的机器人动作安全测试框架，通过联合概率树和因果电路实现拒绝动作的因果诊断与纠正，无需重训练或额外数据收集。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于机器人动作安全测试与因果推理，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：机器人动作安全测试与因果诊断；场景：ROS2仿真环境中的机器人运动规划；传感器类型：未说明；目标类别：未说明。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: Joint Probability Tree (JPT) + Causal Circuit (基于Marginal-Deterministic Variable Tree)。非深度学习模型，不涉及CNN/Transformer等视觉架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关指标。虽然提到"polytime computation"，但这是指因果查询的计算复杂度，非模型部署效率。

Datasets and metrics: 实验环境：ROS2仿真环境；评价指标：失败尝试减少比例（high-quality JPT下减少10.3%，degraded JPT下减少37%）。无遥感数据集。

Experiments: 在ROS2仿真环境中验证框架有效性，展示了因果电路在不同质量JPT下对失败尝试的减少效果，并生成结构化因果报告。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的视觉检测或模型压缩内容。论文面向机器人运动规划安全测试，属于因果推理与机器人学交叉领域。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。因果推理方法本身与遥感目标检测轻量化课题无交集。如果强行关联，因果诊断的思想或许可用于分析轻量化模型在特定场景下的失败模式，但论文未提供任何视觉或遥感领域的验证。

Abstract: Safe physical AI for robot actions are required not only likely to succeed but tested to be safe before execution. In practice, however, formal testing of motion parameters is computationally expensive, and the cost scales poorly with the dimensionality of the action space. When a proposed action is rejected by a tester, the naive response is to resample blindly until a passing candidate is found. This is wasteful, uninformative, and offers no convergence. We argue that rejection should instead trigger causal diagnosis: a principled identification of which action parameter caused the failure and what corrective value maximises the probability of passing testing under the interventional probability distribution. We propose a closed-loop framework that couples a Joint Probability Tree (JPT) with a Causal Circuit derived from a Marginal-Deterministic Variable Tree, enabling exact polytime computation without retraining, or additional data collection. The framework validates tractability of all interventional queries before the robot begins operating, and out-of-support candidates are detected and excluded from correction automatically. We perform experiments in a ROS2 simulation environment, and the framework demonstrates complementary roles across quality of distribution: under a high-quality JPT, the Causal Circuit reduces failed attempts by 10.3% and under a degraded JPT, it reduces total failed attempts by 37%. Every rejected plan produces a structured, interpretable causal report naming the primary cause variable, its observed value, and the recommended corrective region, supporting operator oversight and autonomous recovery without a separately trained failure model.

</details>


### [124] [KineFuse: Kinematic-Aware Haptic Fusion for In-Hand Occluded-Object Pose Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14842)
*Chanyoung Ahn,Jaesung Lee,Sungwoo Park,Donghyun Hwang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种运动学感知的手指级编码器，将稀疏触觉信号（本体感知、力/力矩、二进制接触）与预训练视觉位姿跟踪器融合，解决灵巧手操作中手指遮挡导致的6D位姿跟踪失败问题。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是灵巧手在手指遮挡下的6D位姿跟踪问题，属于机器人操作领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化模型部署。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：6D位姿跟踪（6D pose tracking）；场景：灵巧手在手指遮挡下的在手操作（dexterous in-hand manipulation）；传感器：视觉相机 + 触觉传感器（本体感知、力/力矩、二进制接触）；目标类别：被操作物体（未具体说明）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 提出Kinematic-aware Finger-level Encoder（运动学感知的手指级编码器），与预训练视觉位姿跟踪器融合。对比了四种替代设计。使用跨模态门控（cross-modal gating），视觉专用于平移估计，触觉用一个注意力头专用于旋转估计。采用紧凑的手指级token化（4 tokens）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 评估分为三个层次：逐帧精化（per-frame refinement）、序列开环跟踪（sequential open-loop tracking）、闭环操作（closed-loop manipulation）。有真实世界定性演示。具体数据集名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 主要发现：(i) 逐帧评估无法区分编码器质量，序列跟踪将架构差异放大至15倍；(ii) 结构化编码器学习任务特定跨模态门控，视觉专用于平移，触觉专用于旋转；(iii) 紧凑手指级token化（4 tokens）优于扁平融合和关节级表示。下游重定向任务中跟踪改进带来更高成功率。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无直接可参考的局限性。从自身领域看，摘要未说明在极端遮挡或触觉传感器失效情况下的鲁棒性，也未说明计算开销。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴意义有限。但以下思路可抽象迁移：(1) 多模态融合中的跨模态门控机制——视觉专用于某些任务、触觉专用于其他任务的思想，可类比遥感中光学与SAR的融合；(2) 紧凑token化（4 tokens）优于扁平融合的发现，对遥感轻量模型中的特征压缩策略有启发；(3) 序列评估比逐帧评估更能区分模型质量的实验设计思路，可用于遥感视频流目标跟踪/检测的评估。但这些均为间接借鉴，不直接相关。

Abstract: Dexterous in-hand manipulation requires continuous 6D pose tracking, yet the manipulating fingers inevitably occlude the object from the camera. We study how to structure the sparse haptic signals already available on multi-fingered hands, including proprioception, proximal force/torque, and binary contact, to complement a pretrained visual pose tracker under occlusion. We propose a kinematic-aware finger-level encoder and systematically compare it against four alternative designs through three levels of evaluation: per-frame refinement, sequential open-loop tracking, and closed-loop manipulation. Our experiments reveal that (i) per-frame evaluation cannot distinguish encoder quality, while sequential tracking amplifies architectural differences by up to 15 times; (ii) the structured encoder learns task-specific cross-modal gating, using vision exclusively for translation and dedicating one attention head to haptics for rotation, without explicit supervision; and (iii) compact finger-level tokenization with 4 tokens outperforms both flat fusion and joint-level representations, which suppress vision through norm dominance. We validate that improved tracking yields higher success in a downstream reorientation task and provide qualitative real-world demonstrations. Our project page is available at https://cold-young.github.io/kine-fuse/.

</details>


### [125] [Towards Human-like Physical Intelligence: LifelongVision-Language-Action Learning for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14852)
*Yao He,Gan Sun,Wenqi Liang,Fazeng Li,Yang Cong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向机器人操作的双时间尺度VLA终身学习框架，通过LoRA门控模块和缓存高效重放策略缓解塑性-稳定性权衡问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于机器人操作（robotic manipulation）场景下的Vision-Language-Action (VLA)模型的终身学习问题，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素，也不涉及目标检测任务。虽然文中提到了"轻量级"（lightweight pathways via LoRA）和"低成本部署"（low-cost deployment），但这些是针对机器人场景的，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测的轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、FPGA/NPU部署等）无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议优先阅读。如果对LoRA在持续学习中的应用感兴趣，可作为背景参考，但优先级极低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人操作（robotic manipulation）中的终身学习/持续学习；应用场景：开放世界机器人部署；传感器类型：视觉+语言输入；目标类别：机器人操作技能/行为。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于Vision-Language-Action (VLA)模型，提出双时间尺度LoRA门控模块（dual-timescale LoRA gating module），包含短期适配器（short-term adapter，负责塑性plasticity）和长期适配器（long-term adapter，负责稳定consolidation），通过任务感知门控（task-aware gate）集成。

Lightweight method: 采用LoRA（Low-Rank Adaptation）作为轻量级适配途径；提出缓存高效随机重放策略（cache-efficient stochastic replay strategy），无需存储完整轨迹即可保留平衡的保留信号。但这些轻量化手段是针对机器人终身学习场景设计的，与遥感模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）方法不同。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。仅提到"低成本的机器人部署"（low-cost robotic deployment）和"减少对重训练的依赖"（reduced reliance on retraining），但未给出量化数据。与星上/边缘部署无关。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。仅在实验部分提到在xArm机器人上进行了真实世界部署实验。

Experiments: 实验表明LifelongVLA优于现有基线方法，展示了高效的技能扩展（skill expansion）、对已学操作行为的鲁棒保留（robust retention），以及在xArm机器人上减少了重训练依赖。但未提供与遥感目标检测相关的对比实验。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 摘要未提供量化指标（参数量、FLOPs、延迟等）；3. 仅针对机器人操作场景，未讨论遥感图像特性（如多尺度、旋转目标、背景复杂等）；4. 终身学习设定与遥感在轨模型更新场景有一定概念差异。

Ideas for my research: 虽然论文与遥感方向弱相关，但以下思路可间接借鉴：1. 双时间尺度适配机制（dual-timescale adaptation）的思想——在遥感在轨场景中，可考虑设计短期适配（适应新场景分布漂移）和长期适配（保持已有检测能力）的轻量级更新策略；2. LoRA作为轻量级微调方法，可用于遥感基础模型的在轨参数高效微调；3. 缓存高效重放策略（cache-efficient replay）可用于遥感在轨场景中的增量学习，减少星上存储开销。但需注意，这些借鉴需要重新设计以适应遥感图像特性和目标检测任务。

Abstract: Similar to the natural capabilities of humans to sequentially learn new tasks, robots with Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models should possess lifelong learning ability to learn a new task when deployed in open-world environments. However, most recently proposed lifelong learning models aim to effectively learn the current task (plasticity) or maintain high accuracy on previous tasks (stability), while the plasticity-stability trade-off remains largely unsolved in robotic manipulation models. To address this fundamental challenge, we propose a cache-efficient lifelong Vision-Language-Action learning framework for robotic manipulation (i.e., LifelongVLA), which alleviates the plasticity-stability trade-off with a dual-timescale adaptation mechanism while achieving low-cost robotic deployment with a cache-efficient replay strategy. More concretely, we propose a dual-timescale LoRA gating module to decompose VLA adaptation into two lightweight pathways: a short-term adapter for plasticity and a long-term adapter for stable consolidation. These pathways are integrated via a task-aware gate, enabling explicit control of the plasticity-stability trade-off. In the skill replay phase, a cache-efficient stochastic replay strategy is proposed to preserve more balanced retention signals without full-trajectory storage. Finally, experiments show that LifelongVLA outperforms existing baselines, demonstrating efficient skill expansion, robust retention of learned manipulation behaviors, and reduced reliance on retraining for real-world deployment on an xArm robot.

</details>


### [126] [Modeling and Validation of Quality of Control for Edge-Offloaded Collaborative Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14853)
*Neelabhro Roy,Mikael Hammarling,Victor Nan Fernandez-Ayala,Gourav Prateek Sharma,Mani H. Dhullipalla,Dimos V. Dimarogonas,James Gross*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究复杂环境下协作控制的通信-控制协同设计，通过5G测试实验分析随机无线延迟和可靠性变化对导航、避障等闭环控制性能的影响。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于机器人协作控制中的无线通信延迟与可靠性问题，属于机器人控制与通信协同领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型及其轻量化"课题完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感/卫星相关任务。任务为机器人导航与动态避障中的协作控制，场景为5G无线网络环境下的闭环控制。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明任何深度学习模型或目标检测模型架构。涉及的是控制-通信协同框架（QoC框架）和ROS 2 QoS策略。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅涉及无线通信延迟和可靠性对控制性能的影响。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明遥感或目标检测数据集。评价指标为QoC（quality of control），在私有5G测试平台上进行实验。

Experiments: 在私有5G测试平台上验证了不同延迟、可靠性和控制配置下的QoC性能，比较了ROS 2的RELIABLE QoS和BEST-EFFORT QoS策略，RELIABLE QoS在特定设置下比BEST-EFFORT提升51.5%的QoC。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的模型设计、压缩方法或部署策略。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人控制与无线通信协同领域，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何核心技术。

Abstract: Collaborative control in complex environments is severely challenged by stochastic wireless delay and reliability variations, which can degrade navigation, tracking, and collision avoidance. These network-induced uncertainties complicate the maintenance of energy efficiency during collaborative tasks, and can potentially lead to over-provisioning of resources. In this paper, for a navigation setup with dynamic collision avoidance, we address this challenge by expanding the quality of control (QoC) framework from prior works to practical robotic models. Our approach (i) models end-to-end network effects on closed-loop performance, (ii) systematically explores the impact of various control parameters dictating robotic motion on network latency-reliability (iii) validates these models through experiments on a private 5G testbed across varying delay, reliability and control configurations. Our analysis indicates the optimal control-communication co-design operating regimes for practical robots and also compares the QoC performance of standard ROS~2 quality of service (QoS) policies under real-world conditions and showing how RELIABLE QoS offers 51.5% better QoC than BEST-EFFORT under certain experimental settings.

</details>


### [127] [OASIS-Map: Object-Level Change Detection in Multi-Session Mapping using Semantic Correspondence Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14899)
*Haedam Oh,Yifu Tao,Nived Chebrolu,Maurice Fallon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OASIS-Map，一种基于密集块级语义对应关系的多会话地图构建系统，用于半静态环境中的变化检测和跨访问物体关联。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文聚焦于机器人长期巡检中的地图构建与变化检测，属于机器人/自动驾驶领域的SLAM和场景理解问题，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。不涉及遥感图像、卫星平台、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：多会话地图构建、变化检测、物体关联；场景：半静态真实环境（室内3RScan、停车场、户外市场）；传感器：RGB-D/视觉（摘要未明确说明传感器类型）；目标类别：物体级（家具、车辆等）。

Model architecture: OASIS-Map系统：基于密集块级语义对应（dense patch-level semantic correspondences）建立时间观测间的关联，维护时空一致的物体级地图。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：3RScan（室内物体重排）、停车场（视觉相似车辆替换）、户外市场（大规模场景变化）；评价指标：变化检测F1（0.783）、移动物体关联F1（0.667）。

Experiments: 在三个真实场景上验证：3RScan物体重排、停车场车辆替换、户外市场场景变化。变化检测F1达0.783，物体关联F1达0.667。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/在轨部署完全无关；2. 未涉及任何轻量化或模型压缩技术；3. 未讨论实时推理或资源受限部署；4. 方法依赖密集语义对应，计算开销可能较大。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能相关的点是：变化检测的思想在遥感时序图像分析中有应用（如建筑物变化检测），但本文方法面向机器人SLAM场景，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测的传感器、平台约束、任务目标差异巨大，且未涉及轻量化，不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Map representations which are consistent across repeated visits to a real-world semi-static environment are very useful for long-term robotic inspection. In such settings, the scene may evolve while the robot is absent, with objects appearing, disappearing, moving, or being replaced, quickly making a static map outdated. Existing change-detection methods reason through geometry, category-level semantics, or object persistence. However, achieving reliable object association across revisits remains a key challenge, especially under partial views, occlusion, and imperfect segmentation. In this work, we propose OASIS-Map, a multi-session mapping system that maintains a spatio-temporally consistent object-level map by establishing dense patch-level semantic correspondences between temporal observations. These correspondences detect where the scene has changed and incrementally associate objects across revisits as the robot re-observes the environment. We demonstrate OASIS-Map on three challenging real-world scenarios: object rearrangements in 3RScan, visually similar car replacements in a car park, and large-scale scene changes in an outdoor market. We achieve 0.783 F1 on change detection in a car replacement scenario in a car park and 0.667 F1 on moved object association in 3RScan. https://dynamic.robots.ox.ac.uk/projects/oasis-map/

</details>


### [128] [Human-Robot Interaction in GenAI Architectures via the Agent-Client Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14919)
*Jesus Moncada-Ramirez,Jose-Raul Ruiz-Sarmiento,Javier Gonzalez-Jimenez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Agent-Client Protocol (ACP)的三层解耦架构，用于统一机器人系统中人机交互层的通信标准，实现异构用户界面与机器人平台的互操作性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文讨论的是通用机器人系统中的人机交互层通信协议标准化问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文涉及通用机器人系统的人机交互架构，不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文提出的是三层架构（Human Interaction - Deliberative Orchestration - Physical Execution），属于系统架构层面的设计，非深度学习模型结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文在物理移动机器人上进行了实验验证，但未提及具体数据集或标准评价指标。

Experiments: 在物理移动机器人上验证了三类异构用户界面的互操作性，以及实时人机协同工作流的可行性，延迟开销可忽略不计。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术细节。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人系统架构/HRI领域，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无直接或间接关联。

Abstract: Recent advances in Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI), particularly Large Language Models (LLMs), are driving robotic architectures toward agent-based high-level orchestration, in which natural-language instructions can be translated into context-aware action sequences. While the integration of these agents and robotic capabilities is increasingly converging toward standardization through the Model Context Protocol (MCP), the upper Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) layer remains fragmented by proprietary, ad hoc interfaces that hinder real-time human-in-the-loop collaboration.
  To address this fragmentation, this paper proposes the adoption of the Agent-Client Protocol (ACP) -- a communication standard originally introduced for coding agents in software engineering -- as a unified communication contract for the HRI layer in agent-based robotic systems. By combining ACP at the interface-agent link and MCP at the agent-execution link, we formulate a fully decoupled three-layer architecture that separates human interaction, deliberative orchestration, and physical execution. This topology removes rigid architectural dependencies, enabling heterogeneous user interfaces to connect to the same robotic system and allowing the underlying robotic platform to be replaced without requiring client-specific integration changes. Moreover, it provides native support for collaborative HRI capabilities such as real-time observability, explicit human authorization, and immediate task interruption. We experimentally evaluate the proposed architecture on a physical mobile robot, demonstrating interoperability across three heterogeneous user interfaces and validating real-time human-in-the-loop workflows with negligible latency overhead.

</details>


### [129] [Steering Robustness into World Action Models via Mechanistic Interpretability and Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14943)
*Jihoon Hong,Julian Skifstad,Qiyue Dai,Alice Chan,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究世界动作模型(WAM)在分布偏移下的鲁棒性问题，利用机械可解释性分析激活空间中的鲁棒性表征，提出无训练干预方法和WA-LQR最优控制器来提升模型鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于机器人控制领域的World Action Models的鲁棒性和可解释性，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人控制中的鲁棒性增强与模型干预；应用场景：机器人操作（camera, gripper, visual-noise perturbations）；传感器类型：未明确说明，涉及视觉输入；目标类别：未涉及目标检测。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 涉及WAM架构（Cosmos-Policy, DiT4DiT, LingBot-VA），但摘要未说明具体网络结构细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。

Experiments: 在Cosmos-Policy和DiT4DiT模型上验证了WA-LQR方法，对比了无干预和prompt steering基线，提升了对抗camera, gripper, visual-noise扰动的鲁棒性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或目标检测技术。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于机器人控制/世界模型/可解释性方向，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enable semantically- and physically-informed control but are brittle under distribution shift. In this work, we use mechanistic interpretability to study how robustness-relevant perturbations are represented in WAM activation space. Comparing activations across successful and unsuccessful rollouts, we find some WAM architectures exhibit low-dimensional linear separability for robustness-critical features, while others do not. This motivates the use of contrastive activation directions for training-free WAM steering. We also show that local linearity in WAM activation dynamics enables efficient feedback steering via model-based optimal control, yielding World-Action Linear Quadratic Regulator (WA-LQR), a minimally-invasive reduced-order LQR controller. Via mechanistic evaluations, we predict strong steerability in the Cosmos-Policy and DiT4DiT models but weak steerability in LingBot-VA, consistent with steering intervention results. On Cosmos-Policy and DiT4DiT, WA-LQR generalizes contrastive directions to new tasks and improves robustness to camera, gripper, and visual-noise perturbations over unsteered and prompt steering baselines.

</details>


### [130] [AeroAct: Action-Centered World-Action Models for Language-Conditioned Quadrotor Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14997)
*Xinhong Zhang,Qiyuan Zhu,Yubo Huang,Haolin Chen,Runqing Wang,Yuhao Mo,Zhongxin Chen,Yu Hu,Xinjiang Wang,Jian Sun,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AeroAct，一个基于动作中心世界-动作模型(WAM)的四旋翼飞行器导航方法，利用预训练视频扩散Transformer预测轨迹-动作块，实现语言条件控制的四旋翼飞行。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于四旋翼飞行器(quadrotor)的语言条件导航，属于无人机低空飞行控制领域，不涉及遥感卫星、Earth observation、SAR/高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等核心主题。虽然涉及aerial平台，但场景是低空无人机导航而非卫星遥感对地观测。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题方向基本无关，不推荐阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语言条件四旋翼导航(Language-conditioned quadrotor navigation)；应用场景：低空无人机飞行控制、目标跟踪、目标搜索；传感器类型：第一人称视角相机(egocentric camera)；目标类别：未明确指定具体目标类别。

Model architecture: 基于预训练视频扩散Transformer(video diffusion Transformer)的世界-动作模型(WAM, World-Action Model)，预测局部轨迹-动作块(local trajectory-action chunks)，输入为第一人称视觉历史、本体感知(proprioception)和语言指令。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要提到可以在物理四旋翼飞行器(physical quadrotor)上执行，但未给出参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等具体部署指标。未涉及星上部署、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等边缘部署技术。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：使用DiffAero-based pipeline结合Isaac Lab和3D Gaussian splatting渲染器生成数据，还使用低成本的 handheld collection device 采集飞行类第一人称轨迹数据。评价指标：目标跟踪和目标搜索性能(target tracking and object-search performance)。具体指标名称摘要未说明。

Experiments: 进行了闭环仿真(closed-loop simulation)和真实世界实验(real-world experiments)，结果表明时间视觉上下文(temporal visual context)能提升目标跟踪和目标搜索性能，WAM-based策略可在物理四旋翼上执行。未与现有方法进行定量对比。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及任何轻量化技术；3. 未提供定量指标(参数量、FLOPs、延迟等)；4. 场景为低空无人机飞行控制，非遥感对地观测；5. 未涉及目标检测任务本身。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1. 视频扩散Transformer用于预测未来帧作为监督信号的思想，在遥感视频卫星的时空建模中可能有参考价值；2. 低成本数据采集设备的设计思路可用于遥感数据采集；3. 但整体方向(无人机导航控制)与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化差异较大，不建议深入阅读。

Abstract: Language-conditioned quadrotor flight requires a policy to ground semantic goals, anticipate the visual consequences of ego-motion, and output control references that remain smooth and dynamically executable under rapidly changing first-person views. Existing aerial vision-language navigation and vision-language-action methods commonly use discrete actions, high-level waypoints, or instantaneous velocity commands, which provide limited supervision about how flight actions change future observations. We present AeroAct, an action-centered world-action model (WAM) for quadrotor navigation. To the best of our knowledge, AeroAct is the first WAM instantiated and demonstrated for real-world aerial flight. The model adapts a pretrained video diffusion Transformer to predict local trajectory-action chunks from egocentric visual history, proprioception, and language. Future first-person frames are used during training as dense consequence supervision, while deployment directly decodes actions without generating future video. To obtain aligned visual, state, language, and dynamically feasible action data, we build a DiffAero-based pipeline with complementary Isaac Lab and 3D Gaussian splatting renderers. We further introduce a low-cost handheld collection device that couples camera observations with motion estimates to recreate flight-like egocentric trajectories, and a self-guidance procedure that improves temporal consistency across overlapping trajectory chunks. Closed-loop simulation and real-world experiments show that temporal visual context improves target tracking and object-search performance, and that WAM-based policies can be executed on a physical quadrotor.

</details>


### [131] [CosFly-VLA: A Spatially Aware Vision-Language-Action Model for UAV Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15004)
*Ruilong Ren,Songsheng Cheng,Yunpeng Zhou,Hanxuan Chen,Xiangyue Wang,Tianle Zeng,Shuai Yuan,Binbo Li,Hanzhong Guo,Ji Pei,Da Zhang,Kangli Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CosFly-VLA，一种面向无人机城市动态目标跟踪的视觉-语言-动作模型，通过空间感知预训练、课程学习SFT、CoT推理和闭环RL提升遮挡场景下的跟踪鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 2分（弱相关）。论文涉及无人机（UAV）视角下的目标跟踪，与遥感/卫星图像有一定场景关联，但核心是视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略和遮挡鲁棒跟踪，不涉及遥感卫星在轨目标检测，也不涉及模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Reading priority: 1分（不推荐精读）。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，属于无人机目标跟踪+VLA策略方向，不建议作为核心参考文献。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：动态目标跟踪（dynamic target tracking）；场景：城市复杂环境下的无人机（UAV）视角；传感器类型：可见光相机（RGB）；目标类别：通用地面目标（未明确指定具体类别）。

Model architecture: CosFly-VLA：基于VLA（Vision-Language-Action）架构，包含空间感知预训练、多阶段SFT、CoT推理和闭环RL。模型规模为0.8B参数（0.8 billion）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。模型参数量0.8B，属于中等规模，但未讨论部署优化。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量（除0.8B外）、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性、边缘/星上部署可行性。0.8B参数量的VLA模型在星上部署难度较大，但论文未讨论部署问题。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：500k混合池用于空间预训练（Spatially Grounded CPT）；自然/长遮挡数据用于课程SFT。评价指标：开环ADE（Average Displacement Error）、闭环SR（Success Rate）。

Experiments: 与OpenVLA对比：CosFly-VLA-0.8B在开环ADE上降低34.1%（seen-test）和35.3%（unseen-test）；闭环SR提升29.8%（seen-test）和2.5%（unseen-test）。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测方向无关；2. 未涉及任何轻量化技术；3. 模型规模0.8B，计算开销大，不适合星上部署；4. 仅针对无人机视角，非卫星遥感视角；5. 未讨论SAR、高光谱、多光谱等遥感常用传感器。

Ideas for my research: 1. 课程学习（Curriculum Learning）和CoT推理策略可借鉴用于遥感目标检测的难例挖掘；2. 闭环RL优化思路可用于在轨检测模型的在线自适应；3. 空间感知预训练（depth/distance/3D）对遥感场景理解有启发，但需适配卫星视角；4. 整体而言，该论文与轻量化在轨检测方向关联度低，借鉴价值有限。

Abstract: Dynamic target tracking is essential for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) operating in complex urban environments, where both the target and the camera viewpoint change continuously. Existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies can track visible targets effectively, but their performance often degrades when buildings, vegetation, or roadside objects block the line of sight. During sustained occlusion, a policy may lose the target state, execute actions toward an incorrect region, and amplify this error through subsequent observations until re-acquisition becomes impossible. To this end, we present CosFly-VLA, a spatially aware VLA model that jointly grounds the target, estimates its visibility, and generates continuous flight actions through a structured prediction interface. To train this policy, we use a large-scale recipe over diverse data sources. Spatially Grounded Continued Pretraining (CPT) on a 500k mixed pool injects UAV-view depth, distance, and 3-D spatial reasoning. A three-stage Curriculum-based Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) process then specializes the tracker through multi-head warm-up followed by two-stage curriculum learning over natural and hard / long-occlusion data. Chain-of-Thought (CoT) training subsequently teaches recovery-oriented reasoning traces before structured answers. Finally, a closed-loop Reinforcement Learning (RL) stage optimizes tracking behavior with a multi-component reward covering stand-off tracking, grounding quality, collision avoidance, and task success. Relative to OpenVLA, CosFly-VLA-0.8B reduces open-loop Average Displacement Error (ADE) by 34.1% on seen-test and 35.3% on unseen-test. Closed-loop optimization improves Success Rate (SR) by 29.8% and 2.5%, respectively. These results demonstrate progress from visible-frame imitation toward spatially grounded action-closed-loop control, evaluated under a shared oracle state history.

</details>


### [132] [Risk-Aware Belief Control Barrier Functions over Random Finite Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15016)
*Shaohang Han,Gang Chen,Yixi Cai,Ignacio Torroba,Ivan Stenius,Patric Jensfelt,Javier Alonso-Mora,Jana Tumova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于风险感知信念控制障碍函数（BCBF）的框架，用于在未知动态环境中保障机器人安全，通过随机有限集（RFS）信念和SMC-PHD滤波器处理多目标状态不确定性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于机器人安全控制（robot safety control），涉及多目标状态估计与信念控制障碍函数，属于机器人控制/规划领域，与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化均无直接关联。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：机器人安全控制（safe control）；场景：未知动态环境（unknown, dynamic environments），水下实验（underwater experiments）；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：动态移动物体（moving objects）。

Model architecture: 核心框架：风险感知信念控制障碍函数（BCBF）；状态估计：序贯蒙特卡洛概率假设密度滤波器（SMC-PHD filter），基于随机有限集（RFS）理论。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时推理或边缘/星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 实验方式：仿真（simulation）和真实水下实验（real-world underwater experiments）；具体数据集名称和评价指标：摘要未说明。

Experiments: 通过仿真和水下实验验证了所提方法的有效性和效率（effectiveness and efficiency），但未给出定量对比结果。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无借鉴意义。摘要未讨论计算资源约束、模型大小、推理速度等部署关键问题。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于机器人安全控制领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题的研究目标、技术路线和应用场景均不匹配。

Abstract: Ensuring robot safety in unknown, dynamic environments is a fundamental requirement. It involves inferring the states of an unknown and time-varying number of moving objects from noisy, incomplete measurements. We address safe control under the induced multi-object state uncertainty with a risk-aware belief control barrier function (BCBF) framework. The uncertainty is captured by a random finite set (RFS) belief, estimated by a sequential Monte Carlo probability hypothesis density (SMC-PHD) filter that represents it with a set of particles. Building directly on these particles, we construct a nonsmooth BCBF, establish forward invariance of the safe set under continuous prediction, and derive an explicit condition under which discrete updates preserve safety. Simulation and real-world underwater experiments demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [133] [Learning Agile Navigation in Crowded Environments for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15036)
*Shuyu Wu,Zeyu Liu,Tianbao Zhang,Fanxing Li,Fangyu Sun,Mingkang Xiong,Wei Xi,Wenxian Yu,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VOP-Nav，将Velocity Obstacles的几何安全性与端到端学习的适应性结合，用于四足机器人在动态拥挤环境中的导航，避免显式目标检测与跟踪。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦四足机器人导航（quadruped robot navigation），传感器为LiDAR，场景为动态拥挤地面环境，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感核心要素，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化模型压缩方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：四足机器人动态避障导航；场景：室内外动态拥挤环境；传感器：LiDAR（多帧点云）；目标类别：行人/障碍物（隐式编码，无显式检测）。

Model architecture: VOP-Net：处理多帧LiDAR数据，隐式编码动态约束并预测安全速度区域（基于Velocity Obstacle理论）。策略网络使用VO预测作为输入，训练时VO预测同时作为奖励信号。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等）。

Onboard deployability: 在Unitree Go2四足机器人上进行了真实部署，但摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等具体部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 仿真环境：Isaac Gym；真实部署：Unitree Go2；评价指标：成功率（success rate）、 locomotion speed、碰撞避免能力。摘要未说明具体数据集名称。

Experiments: 在Isaac Gym中与多种基线方法对比，VOP-Nav取得更高成功率；在Unitree Go2真实机器人上验证了鲁棒性和效率。摘要未给出消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关，无法直接借鉴；2. 摘要未说明模型计算复杂度、参数量等关键指标；3. 仅针对地面四足机器人场景，不涉及星上资源约束。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接启发：将几何先验（如VO）与学习结合的双重利用方式（同时作为输入和奖励信号）的思路，在遥感目标检测中可类比为将物理先验（如目标尺寸分布、轨道几何约束）融入轻量检测网络的训练与推理。

Abstract: Navigating dynamic and crowded environments presents significant challenges for quadruped robots due to severe sensor occlusion and unpredictable human motion. Existing approaches face a trade-off: model-based methods, such as Velocity Obstacles (VO), theoretically guarantee safety but rely on accurate obstacle motion estimates that often fail in dense crowds, while end-to-end learning methods offer robustness but lack motion prediction capability of obstacles, leading to collisions or conservative behaviors. To solve this, we propose VOP-Nav, a novel navigation system that combines the geometric safety of VO with the agile adaptability of end-to-end learning. Using only local onboard observations, our system avoids explicit obstacle detection and tracking pipelines. The VOP-Net processes multi-frame LiDAR data to implicitly encode dynamic constraints and predict a safe velocity region derived from Velocity Obstacle theory. Importantly, the VO predictions serve a dual role: they are used as input to the navigation policy during inference and as a reward signal during training to encourage safe motion. Evaluations in Isaac Gym demonstrate that VOP-Nav achieves higher success rates than all baselines while balancing locomotion speed and collision avoidance. Real-world deployment on a Unitree Go2 quadruped robot further validates the system's robustness and efficiency in complex indoor and outdoor dynamic environments.

</details>


### [134] [DriftWorld: Fast World Modeling through Drifting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15065)
*Susie Lu,Haonan Chen,Weirui Ye,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DriftWorld，一种基于漂移生成模型的动作条件世界模型，通过单次前向传播替代扩散模型的多步迭代去噪，实现30+ fps的快速未来帧生成，用于机器人规划与控制。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于机器人操控（robotic manipulation）的世界模型与规划，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化模型压缩方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）。虽然其"单次前向传播替代迭代采样"的思路在加速推理层面有广义借鉴意义，但场景、任务、数据均与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无关。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化四个关键词均无直接关联，属于机器人规划领域的工作，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人世界模型（world model）用于动作规划与控制；应用场景：视觉机器人操控（vision-based robotic manipulation）；传感器类型：RGB相机；目标类别：机器人操作对象（如推块、抓取等），非遥感目标。摘要未说明遥感相关场景。

Model architecture: DriftWorld基于漂移生成模型（drifting generative models），学习动作条件漂移（action-conditioned drift），在训练阶段完成漂移学习，推理时从当前观测和候选动作序列单次前向传播生成未来帧。与扩散模型（diffusion model）的多步迭代去噪路径不同。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等传统轻量化方法。其加速策略是模型架构层面的设计选择（用漂移模型替代扩散模型），而非模型压缩或轻量化技术。推理速度提升（17x faster, 30+ fps）是架构差异带来的，不是轻量化方法的结果。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及推理速度30+ fps，比扩散基线平均快17倍。但该速度是在机器人操控场景下报告的，与遥感卫星在轨部署（星上计算资源受限、实时性要求高）无直接可比性。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：Bridge-V2, RT-1, Language Table, Push-T, Robomimic（均为机器人操控基准数据集，非遥感数据集）。评价指标：决策性能（state-of-the-art decision-making performance）、推理时间（inference time）、离线仿真中rollout分数与真实策略的相关性（correlation up to 0.99）。

Experiments: 在多个机器人操控基准上评估，DriftWorld在决策性能上达到SOTA，推理时间远低于基于扩散的世界模型基线。离线仿真中rollout分数与真实策略相关性高达0.99。摘要未说明消融实验或对比方法的详细结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，场景、任务、数据均不匹配；2. 未涉及任何模型轻量化技术（剪枝、量化、蒸馏等）；3. 未报告参数量、FLOPs等部署关键指标；4. 漂移模型在遥感图像（高分辨率、多光谱、大尺寸）上的适用性未知；5. 未讨论在边缘/星上计算资源受限环境下的部署可行性。

Ideas for my research: 虽然论文与课题方向弱相关，但以下思路有广义借鉴价值：1. "用单次前向传播替代迭代采样"的加速思想，可类比思考遥感目标检测中是否可以用类似策略替代某些迭代式后处理（如NMS的加速变体）或迭代式检测头；2. 推理速度提升17x的幅度提示，在遥感在轨场景中，架构级创新（而非仅压缩已有模型）可能带来更大的加速收益。但整体而言，该论文对"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接可借鉴点非常有限。

Abstract: Predictive world models enable robots to plan by imagining the outcomes of their actions, but their value for control hinges on generating many rollouts quickly. This creates a bottleneck for diffusion-based world models: multistep sampling makes each rollout expensive, limiting large-scale action search at inference time. We introduce DriftWorld, an action-conditioned world model based on drifting generative models. Rather than denoising iteratively at inference, DriftWorld learns an action-conditioned drift during training, allowing it to generate future frames from the current observation and a candidate action sequence in a single forward pass at 30+ fps, which is 17x faster on average than diffusion based baselines. We evaluate DriftWorld on standard vision-based robotic manipulation benchmarks, including Bridge-V2, RT-1, Language Table, Push-T, and Robomimic. By producing rollouts that are both accurate and fast, DriftWorld achieves state-of-the-art decision-making performance with far less inference time than diffusion-based world model baselines. Beyond online control, DriftWorld can also serve as an offline simulator for ranking real-world robot policies, with rollout-based scores correlating with ground truth at up to 0.99. These results show that drifting models are a strong fit for robot world modeling, where fast, high-quality imagination directly supports planning and policy evaluation.

</details>


### [135] [Goal-Oriented Semantic Communication for Distributed ISAC-Enabled Vehicle Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15111)
*Wenjie Liu,Yansha Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向无信号灯路口的分布式ISAC车辆协调框架，采用目标导向语义通信和MHPPO强化学习，实现碰撞避免与信令开销降低。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、航空图像、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于智能交通系统中的车辆通信与协调问题。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：车辆状态感知与通信协同控制；场景：无信号灯路口（智能交通）；传感器：RSU（路侧单元）感知信号；目标：车辆状态获取与运动控制。与遥感卫星场景无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体检测或视觉模型架构。提出MHPPO（Masked Hybrid Proximal Policy Optimization）强化学习框架用于联合决策感知传输、C&C传输及信号内容。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文关注的是通信信令效率（语义通信、VoI奖励驱动的选择性传输），而非模型轻量化（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等部署指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。评价指标：碰撞率（100%无碰撞）、信令开销（signaling overhead reduction）。

Experiments: 仿真实验，与预测性ISAC基线及多个消融基线对比，实现100%无碰撞且信令开销显著降低。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型压缩或部署技术。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于通信与控制领域，不涉及遥感图像目标检测或模型轻量化。

Abstract: Vehicle coordination at unsignalized intersections relies on accurate real-time vehicle state acquisition and reliable command-and-control (C&C) signal delivery. However, existing studies typically treat sensing, communication, and control separately, which may lead to redundant transmissions, outdated state information, and unreliable vehicle coordination. In this paper, we investigate a new scenario of distributed integrated sensing and communication (ISAC)-enabled vehicle coordination at intersections, where multiple roadside units (RSUs) collaboratively transmit sensing signals for vehicle state acquisition and C&C signals for vehicle movement control under the management of a central base station (BS). To improve signaling efficiency, we propose a unified goal-oriented semantic communication (GSC) framework, which transmits sensing and C&C signals only when they are semantically important for improving intersection traffic throughput. Specifically, an extended Kalman filter (EKF) is adopted to predict vehicle states and fuse distributed sensing measurements. A masked hybrid proximal policy optimization (MHPPO) framework is then developed to jointly determine sensing transmission decisions, C&C transmission decisions, and C&C signal contents based on a value-of-information (VoI) reward. Furthermore, we propose an uncertainty-aware transmission design (UTD), including robust beamforming and VoI-based time-division power allocation, to improve sensing and communication reliability under vehicle state uncertainty and inter-RSU interference. Simulation results show that our proposed framework achieves 100% collision-free vehicle coordination with significantly reduced signaling overhead compared with predictive ISAC baselines adapted from state-of-the-art related studies and several ablation baselines.

</details>


### [136] [Catch, Throw, Repeat: Planning for Human-Robot Partner Juggling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15129)
*Jonathan Rainer Lippert,Kai Ploeger,Abir Chowdhury,Hermann Müller,Jan Peters,Alap Kshirsagar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种人机协作抛接球（partner juggling）的实时规划与控制架构，实现机器人与人类在多球模式下的同步抛接。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是人机物理交互中的动态物体交换（抛接球），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型及其轻量化"课题完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：人机协作抛接球（human-robot partner juggling）；应用场景：物理人机交互；传感器类型：视觉追踪（具体传感器摘要未说明）；目标类别：球（balls）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 实时规划与控制架构，包含：预测性球追踪（predictive ball tracking）、自适应在线轨迹优化（adaptive online trajectory optimization，使用multiple-shooting formulation）、基于状态机的协调逻辑（state-machine-based coordination logic）。不涉及任何视觉检测模型或神经网络架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。仅提及"实时"（real-time），但未给出具体延迟或帧率数据。

Datasets and metrics: 用户研究（user study）：8名参与者，技能从新手到专家。评价指标：连续接球次数（consecutive catches）、成功率（success rate）。与遥感标准数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR等）无关。

Experiments: 所有参与者均在10分钟测试中超过此前报道的最佳结果；一名参与者将三球级联抛接记录提升5倍至20次连续接球；另一名参与者在单球接返任务中实现40次连续接球、100%成功率。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的模型压缩、检测或遥感场景信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人控制与人机交互领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Abstract: Dynamic object exchange between humans and robots remains a challenging problem due to uncertainty in perception, timing, and contact-rich interaction. Human-robot juggling represents a particularly demanding instance of this problem, requiring precise real-time coordination, predictive motion planning with feedback control, and robustness to variability in human motion. Enabling such skills is of interest for advancing physical human-robot interaction and shared autonomy. We present a real-time planning and control architecture for human-robot partner juggling that enables a robot to reliably catch and throw balls in synchronized multi-ball patterns with a human partner. The system integrates predictive ball tracking, adaptive online trajectory optimization using a multiple-shooting formulation, and a state-machine-based coordination logic to enable synchronized multi-ball human-robot partner juggling. In a user study with 8 participants of varying juggling skill from beginner to expert, we demonstrate that our system can achieve three-ball cascades shared between the robot and the human. All participants exceeded previously reported best-case results within a 10-minute test session, with one participant extending the previous record for shared three-ball cascade juggling fivefold to 20 consecutive robot catches, and another participant achieving a 100% success rate with 40 consecutive catches in a single-ball catch-and-return setting. Video documentation can be found at https://kai-ploeger.com/partner-juggling

</details>


### [137] [Assessing Physical Frailty and Fall-Risk Indicators with Social Robots: An in situ Evaluation with Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15156)
*Aniol Civit,Antonio Andriella,Alba Martínez,Joan Ars,Aida Ribera,Cristian Barrué,Guillem Alenyà*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个基于行为树架构的社交机器人框架，通过视觉骨架追踪引导老年人完成标准化衰弱评估测试（SPPB、TUG），实现临床评分与额外运动指标的自动采集。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于医疗康复场景下的老年人衰弱评估，使用社交机器人和视觉骨架追踪，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感领域内容，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：老年人衰弱评估与跌倒风险评估；应用场景：医疗康复中心；传感器类型：RGB摄像头（视觉骨架追踪）、IMU、步态分析走道；目标类别：人体骨架关键点、步态参数。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于行为树（Behaviour Tree）架构，协调感知、决策、交互和测量模块。视觉部分使用基于骨架跟踪（vision-based skeleton tracking）的方法。摘要未说明具体网络结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时推理或边缘/星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：在康复中心研究实验室6个月内采集，N=81名老年人；评价指标：组内相关系数（ICC）、Cohen's Kappa系数（k）；临床测试：SPPB（Short Physical Performance Battery）、TUG（Timed Up and Go）。

Experiments: 机器人测量与治疗师评估及临床参考仪器（步态分析走道、IMU）对比。大多数测试完成时间和步态参数ICC>0.9；SPPB总分与治疗师比较k=0.67（substantial agreement），与IMU比较k=0.55（moderate agreement）。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可参考的轻量化或目标检测技术细节。摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理速度等部署相关信息。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于医疗机器人/HRI领域，不涉及遥感、目标检测或模型轻量化，对"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题无参考价值。

Abstract: Frailty assessments are crucial to evaluate the risk of adverse events and the health and social care needs of older adults, yet their administration remains resource-intensive and typically relies on coarse clinical outcomes, such as task completion times, which may overlook biomechanical indicators of functional decline. To address this, we present a robotic framework that guides older adults through standardised frailty and fall-risk tests while capturing clinical scores and additional frailty-related metrics, offering a deeper insight into a user's condition. The system uses a Behaviour Tree architecture that coordinates perception, decision-making, interaction, and measurement modules. Using vision-based skeleton tracking, the robot evaluates established clinical tests, including the Short Physical Performance Battery (SPPB) and the Timed Up and Go (TUG). The framework was co-designed with healthcare professionals and evaluated in situ during six months in a rehabilitation centre's research lab with N=81 older adults. Robot-derived measurements were compared against therapist assessments and clinical reference instruments, including a gait analysis walkway and an inertial measurement unit (IMU). Results showed excellent agreement for most test completion times and gait-related parameters ($ICC > 0.9$). And, substantial agreement for the overall SPPB score comparing the robot and the therapist ($k = 0.67$) and moderate agreement comparing the robot and the IMU ($k=0.55$). The findings highlight that social robots can provide reliable and objective frailty assessments in healthcare settings while enabling the collection of relevant mobility indicators beyond conventional outcomes.

</details>


### [138] [Scaling Behavior Foundation Model for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15163)
*Weishuai Zeng,Kangning Yin,Xiaojie Niu,Shunlin Lu,Weixiang Zhong,Jiahe Chen,Feiyu Jia,Xiao Chen,Zirui Wang,Furui Xu,Ming Zhou,Kailin Li,Weinan Zhang,He Wang,Li Yi,Dahua Lin,Jiangmiao Pang,Jingbo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出面向人形机器人控制的Behavior Foundation Model (BFM)，通过运动跟踪学习范式、策略数据与参考动作多样性协同、Humanoid Transformer架构三方面协调实现可扩展的人形控制。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全聚焦于人形机器人控制（humanoid control），与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等方向毫无关联。不涉及任何遥感目标检测或轻量化部署内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：人形机器人全身协调控制（humanoid whole-body control）；应用场景：仿真环境与真实世界人形机器人部署；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：人形机器人姿态关键点（keypoints）。

Model architecture: Humanoid Transformer，一种可扩展的模型架构，促进结构化行为表征的自然涌现。具体结构细节摘要未说明。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文讨论的是真实世界人形机器人部署，而非星上/边缘部署。未提供参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：摘要未说明具体数据集名称；评价指标：Mean Per-Keypoint Position Error (MPKPE)，分为local mode和global mode。

Experiments: 仿真与真实世界部署实验。相比已有的人形控制器，在测试集上local mode MPKPE降低超过10%，global mode MPKPE降低82%。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或遥感相关技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人控制领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题方向无交集。

Abstract: Humanoid control requires natural whole-body coordination, precise real-time responses to control signals, and robust generalization across diverse environmental contexts, making it a cornerstone for generalist embodied agents. Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) have recently emerged as a promising solution to address these challenges by leveraging large-scale behavioral data to achieve superior expressiveness, versatility and generalization. However, despite growing interest in scaling BFMs to further improve their capabilities, it remains unclear how key factors, including the learning paradigm, behavioral data and model architecture should be coordinated to enable effective scaling. In this work, we revisit the scaling recipe for BFMs and demonstrate that substantial performance gains can be achieved through the coordination of three core components: 1) the learning paradigm of motion tracking that reformulates diverse humanoid control problems as the reproduction of integrated whole-body behaviors in the global frame; 2) the strategic synergy between on-policy rollout quantity and reference motion diversity; and 3) the expressive and scalable model architecture termed Humanoid Transformer that facilitates the natural emergence of structured behavioral representations. Through extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world deployment, we demonstrate that our approach yields significant improvements in control fidelity and task generalization, reducing Mean Per-Keypoint Position Error (MPKPE) on the test set by over 10% in local mode and 82% in global mode compared with existing humanoid controllers. These results establish BFM as a principled and effective foundation for scalable and general-purpose humanoid control.

</details>


### [139] [AHEAD: Anticipatory Hand-Driven Teleoperation via Human Intent Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15172)
*Seok Joon Kim,Junho Lee,Federica Spinola,Taein Kwon,Mohsen Moghaddam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AHEAD，一种基于手部/头部信号与场景上下文的注意力分类器预测操作意图的VR遥操作系统，用于减少机器人反应延迟和操作员负荷。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文研究的是VR遥操作机器人系统，涉及意图预测、人机交互、机器人控制，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：VR遥操作机器人抓取与放置（pick-and-place）；场景：数字孪生环境中的手部遥操作；传感器：3D手部/头部信号（非遥感传感器）；目标类别：抓取物体与放置槽位。

Model architecture: 基于注意力的分类器（attention-based classifier），处理3D手部/头部信号与场景上下文；状态机将意图预测转换为稳定的机器人目标。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、能耗、延迟等部署指标。仅提及机器人反应延迟减少0.6s（物体）和1.4s（槽位），但这是系统层面的延迟，非模型推理延迟。

Datasets and metrics: 用户实验（user study）；指标：Top1准确率（抓取物体76%、放置槽位76%）、机器人反应延迟、操作员负荷（主观报告）。

Experiments: 意图预测模块Top1准确率：抓取物体76%、放置槽位76%；用户实验显示相比基线减少0.6s（物体）和1.4s（槽位）反应延迟；操作员负荷降低。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关，无任何可迁移的技术方法或实验设置。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于机器人遥操作/人机交互领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化课题无技术交集。

Abstract: Direct hand-driven teleoperation maps an operator's hand motion to robot end-effector commands at every frame, enabling precise control, but it requires constant monitoring and correction during approach, grasp, and placement, which can be slow and fatiguing. For repetitive pick-and-place tasks, supervisory (goal-based) teleoperation simplifies this process: the operator specifies goals/waypoints, and the robot executes the motion using planning algorithms. Yet, this introduces latency, as the robot must wait for the next command before it can plan and act. "How can we reduce robot reaction time while lowering operator workload?" To tackle this question, we present AHEAD, a real-time VR teleoperation system that anticipates operator intent to enable proactive, hand-driven control. In a digital twin, the operator performs pick-and-place naturally, using hand motion to convey high-level commands rather than a continuous robot trajectory. AHEAD processes a short window of 3D hand and head signals together with scene context through an attention-based classifier to predict the intended grasp object and placement slot. A state machine converts intent predictions into stable robot goals, enabling early motion while remaining stable under noisy predictions and corrective hand movements. AHEAD's intent prediction module achieves Top1 accuracy: 76% for grasp objects and 76% for target slots. Moreover, our user study shows AHEAD reduces robot reaction latency by 0.6 s (object) and 1.4 s (slot) relative to baselines. Participants also reported lower operator load, indicating faster robot responses while maintaining low operator effort in practice.

</details>


### [140] [RoboTTT: Context Scaling for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15275)
*Yunfan Jiang,Yevgen Chebotar,Ruijie Zheng,Fengyuan Hu,Yunhao Ge,Jimmy Wu,Tianyuan Dai,Scott Reed,Li Fei-Fei,Yuke Zhu,Linxi "Jim" Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RoboTTT，一种将机器人策略的视觉运动上下文扩展到8000时间步的测试时训练方法，用于机器人操作任务。

Research relevance: 与本研究方向基本无关（1分）。论文聚焦于机器人操作（robot manipulation）和视觉-语言-动作策略（Vision-Language-Action policies），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分（最低优先级）。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化均无直接关系，属于机器人操作领域的工作，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：机器人操作策略（robot manipulation policy）；应用场景：真实机器人操作任务（real-robot manipulation tasks），如多阶段长时程装配任务；传感器类型：视觉（visuomotor context）；目标类别：无目标检测，涉及机器人动作执行。与遥感目标检测完全无关。

Model architecture: 基于Vision-Language-Action (VLA)策略的机器人基础模型，集成Test-Time Training (TTT)，将梯度下降更新的快速权重（fast weights）作为循环状态，实现长上下文条件化。训练采用sequence action forcing + truncated backpropagation through time。与遥感目标检测模型架构无关。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等）。虽然TTT本身是一种在测试时进行参数更新的方法，但论文并未讨论模型压缩或加速部署。与轻量化方向无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、显存、延迟、能耗等部署相关信息。论文提到"without growing inference latency"但未给出具体数值。完全不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：真实机器人操作任务（具体数据集名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：任务完成率（overall performance improvement by 87%），五阶段十步装配任务完成情况。与遥感数据集（如DOTA、HRSC2016、DIOR、NWPU VHR-10等）完全无关。

Experiments: 主要结果：相比单步上下文基线提升87%性能；8K时间步上下文相比1K时间步预训练提升62%；完整完成5分钟10阶段装配任务（基线从未完成）。消融实验摘要未详细说明。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供直接借鉴。局限性包括：仅针对机器人操作场景验证；TTT在推理时进行梯度更新可能带来额外计算开销，与轻量化目标相悖。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关借鉴点：（1）Test-Time Training的思想——在测试阶段利用少量样本更新模型参数，可类比于遥感卫星在轨场景中利用新获取的遥感图像进行模型自适应更新；（2）长上下文建模的思路——遥感视频流或时序遥感图像中的目标检测可借鉴长时序上下文建模方法。但整体而言，该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题的直接可借鉴性很低。

Abstract: Recent robot foundation models operate with single-step or short-history visuomotor context. We introduce Test-Time-Training Robot Policies (RoboTTT), a robot model and training recipe that scale visuomotor context to 8K timesteps, three orders of magnitude beyond state-of-the-art policies, without growing inference latency. At this context length, we unlock new robot capabilities: one-shot in-context imitation from human video demonstrations, on-the-fly policy improvement, robustness to perturbations, and stronger performance on multi-stage, long-horizon tasks. We also observe, for the first time, steady gains in closed-loop performance as pretraining context length scales. At its core, RoboTTT integrates Test-Time Training into robot foundation models such as Vision-Language-Action policies, yielding a sequence model whose recurrent state consists of fast weights, parameters updated by gradient descent during both training and inference, compressing histories into weight space and retrieving contextual information for long-context conditioning. To scale training context length, the recipe combines sequence action forcing with truncated backpropagation through time. On challenging real-robot manipulation tasks, RoboTTT improves overall performance by 87% over the single-step context baseline and fully completes a five-minute, ten-stage assembly task, which no baseline ever does. RoboTTT trained with 8K-timestep context outperforms the same model pretrained with 1K timesteps by 62%, suggesting context length as a new scaling axis for robot foundation models. Videos are available at https://research.nvidia.com/labs/gear/robottt/

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

### [141] [OvAi Focus: AI-based Multi-class Segmentation of Functional Ovaries and Adnexal Masses in Gynecological Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14179)
*Niccolò Tallone,Francesca Salis,Pio Raffaele Fina,Roberta Massobrio,Rosilari Bellacosa Marotti,Daniele Conti,Luca Fuso,Luca Mariani,Annamaria Ferrero,Alessandro Arena,Stefano Cosma,Dan Grisaru,Angelo Lacalandra,Renato Seracchioli,Marianna Roccio,Federica Gerace*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 一篇面向卵巢癌超声图像分割的AI医疗软件论文，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是医学超声图像中的卵巢和附件肿块分割，属于医学影像AI领域。完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题；不涉及目标检测（而是语义分割）；不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署、星上部署等任何模型压缩或部署优化技术。与本研究方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多类语义分割（multi-class semantic segmentation）；应用场景：卵巢癌超声诊断；传感器类型：超声（US）；目标类别：功能性卵巢、附件肿块（囊性/实性成分）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：多中心数据集，1,081名成年女性，来自意大利和以色列6个中心；评价指标：DICE系数（完整病变0.87，囊性0.85，实性0.68，功能性卵巢0.62）。

Experiments: 在异质采集设置下，分割性能达到或优于现有方法。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术信息。摘要未说明模型复杂度、推理速度等关键信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于医学影像分割领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化课题的研究场景、任务类型、技术路线均不相关。

Abstract: Ovarian cancer is the deadliest gynecological malignancy; accurate and objective segmentation of adnexal masses and functional ovaries in ultrasound (US) remains challenging due to operator variability and morphological complexity. We present OvAi Focus (SynDiag s.r.l., Italy), a stand-alone AI software medical device that performs multi-class semantic segmentation of functional ovaries and adnexal masses, distinguishing cystic from solid components. The system was trained and independently validated on a multicenter dataset of 1,081 adult women from 6 centers across Italy and Israel. Segmentation achieved DICE scores of 0.87 (complete lesion), 0.85 (cystic), 0.68 (solid), and 0.62 (functional ovary), in line with or superior to state-of-the-art approaches across heterogeneous acquisition settings.

</details>


### [142] [A Hybrid Framework for Blood Vessel Morphology Classification: Discrete Geometry-based Tortuosity Feature Measurement, Information Gain-based Feature Selection, and Random Forest Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14195)
*Yu Zhong,Jingzhi Guo,Luyao Li,Zehao Wang,Zhihui Yang,Yixin Lin,Weilun Fu,Yang Wang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出基于离散几何特征、信息增益特征选择和随机森林的颈内动脉形态分类框架，用于血管迂曲度的客观量化分级。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是医学影像中的血管形态分类（颈内动脉迂曲度分级），完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等遥感领域内容；也不涉及目标检测、小目标检测、旋转框检测等检测任务；更不涉及轻量化、剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘/星上部署等模型压缩与加速技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：血管形态分类（二元/三元分级）；应用场景：临床脑血管评估与中风风险评价；传感器类型：医学影像（血管中心线）；目标类别：颈内动脉C1段迂曲度（非严重/严重，直/低迂曲/高迂曲）。

Model architecture: 离散几何特征提取 + 信息增益特征选择 + Random Forest分类器。初始13维迂曲特征经特征选择缩减为6维子集（TI, AC, TC, AC/AT, AT, TT）。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。未使用剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等任何模型压缩或加速方法。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：379条临床血管中心线；评价指标：Macro-F1分数；实验设置：二元分类（非严重/严重迂曲）和三元分类（直/低迂曲/高迂曲）。

Experiments: 二元分类Macro-F1=0.9206，三元分类Macro-F1=0.8626。特征重要性分析表明伸长和曲率相关特征对基础筛查贡献大，扭转相关特征对精细分类有额外信息。定义了Morphological Risk Index (MRI)。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的轻量化或部署信息。医学影像领域方法，数据模态、任务目标、评价体系均与遥感目标检测差异巨大。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值极低。唯一可能参考的是其"特征选择"思路（Information Gain-based feature selection），在遥感轻量化中可类比为通道剪枝或特征维度压缩，但该论文使用的是传统机器学习而非深度学习，且不涉及任何部署约束，参考意义有限。

Abstract: Subjective visual grading of blood vessel tortuosity relies heavily on clinical experience, while traditional distance-based indices often fail to adequately characterize three-dimensional spatial deformation. Because abnormal internal carotid artery morphology may be clinically relevant to cerebrovascular assessment and stroke-risk evaluation, objective and reproducible quantification of vascular tortuosity is of considerable importance. To address this limitation, we propose a mathematical framework for the morphological classification of the internal carotid artery (ICA-C1) segment. The framework integrates discrete geometric feature measurement, Information Gain-based feature selection, and Random Forest classification. An initial set of 13 tortuosity features is extracted from the corresponding 379 clinical vascular centerlines using discrete geometric methods and subsequently reduced to a six-feature subset consisting of $\mathcal{TI}$, $\mathcal{AC}$, $\mathcal{TC}$, $\mathcal{AC}/\mathcal{AT}$, $\mathcal{AT}$, and $\mathcal{TT}$. The framework is evaluated in two classification tasks. For binary classification of non-severe and severe tortuosity, the RF model achieves a Macro-F1 score of 0.9206. For ternary morphological grading into straight, low-tortuosity, and high-tortuosity groups, it achieves a Macro-F1 score of 0.8626. The results indicate that elongation- and curvature-related features provide strong discriminatory information for basic screening, whereas torsion-related features contribute additional information for more detailed morphological classification. Based on the RF feature-importance values, we further define a Morphological Risk Index (MRI), which provides a direct numerical reference for vascular morphology and may facilitate more objective and consistent clinical assessment.

</details>


### [143] [FORCE-Interior: A Poisson Flow Generative Prior for Interior Tomography Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14320)
*Kang Chen,Wenjun Xia,Jianxu Wang,Mahmud Wasif Nafee,Ge Wang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出FORCE-Interior，一种基于泊松流生成模型的内部断层重建框架，用于解决投影截断导致的ROI重建病态问题。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是医学CT（计算机断层扫描）中的interior tomography（内部断层重建）问题，属于医学影像领域，与遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等方向完全无关。不涉及目标检测，也不涉及任何轻量化方法。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：医学CT图像重建（interior tomography）；应用场景：医学影像；传感器类型：CT扫描仪；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。与遥感场景无关。

Model architecture: 基于Poisson-flow生成模型（Poisson-flow generative model），结合full-FOV测量约束初始化与逐步骤数据一致性约束。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未明确说明具体数据集名称。评价指标涉及结构重建质量（structural quality）和感知重建质量（perceptual reconstruction quality）以及投影域一致性（projection-domain consistency）。

Experiments: 实验表明FORCE-Interior在两种更严重截断的ROI尺寸上取得了更好的结构和感知重建质量，在最大ROI尺寸上结果具有竞争力，同时保持了投影域一致性。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关；2. 未讨论计算效率或模型压缩；3. 仅针对医学CT场景设计，泛化到其他模态未说明。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无直接可借鉴点。唯一可能的间接启发是：生成模型中的数据一致性约束思想（data consistency）在遥感图像恢复/超分任务中可能有参考价值，但与目标检测轻量化无关。

Abstract: Interior tomography reconstructs a region of interest (ROI) from truncated projection measurements. However, projection truncation makes the inverse problem severely ill-posed, leading to non-unique solutions, oversmoothing, and truncation-induced artifacts when conventional reconstruction methods are directly applied to interior tomography. Existing learning-based interior CT methods have shown promising performance, but their generalization across different truncation patterns, ROI sizes, and noise levels remains an important challenge. Meanwhile, current generative model-based reconstruction methods are primarily designed for non-interior tomography settings and do not directly address ROI-based projection truncation. Moreover, without sufficient data-consistency constraints, generative sampling may yield anatomically plausible but measurement-inconsistent structures. To address these challenges, we propose FORCE-Interior, a Poisson-flow generative reconstruction framework for interior tomography. FORCE-Interior combines a full-FOV measurement-constrained initialization with per-step data consistency for ROI-truncated measurements, anchoring generative sampling to the acquired measurements throughout reconstruction. Experiments show that FORCE-Interior achieves improved structural and perceptual reconstruction quality at the two more severely truncated ROI sizes, with competitive results at the largest, while maintaining projection-domain consistency.

</details>


### [144] [ViPSAM: Visual Prompting Medical Image Segmentation Using Segment Anything Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14328)
*San Lee,Nalee Kim,Jeong Il Yu,Hee Chul Park,Boah Kim*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出ViPSAM，一种基于视觉提示的跨模态分割框架，利用对比增强MRI的引导信息辅助非增强CT上的病灶分割，基于SAM模型进行参数高效微调。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文面向质子治疗中的医学图像分割（肝脏病灶分割），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱等遥感场景，也不涉及目标检测或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测与轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：医学图像分割（lesion segmentation）；应用场景：质子治疗规划（proton therapy planning）；传感器/模态：非增强CT（NCCT）和对比增强MRI；目标类别：肝脏病灶（liver lesion）。与遥感目标检测无关。

Model architecture: 基于Segment Anything Model (SAM)，引入visual prompt encoder提取对比增强图像的引导特征，以及visual-guided cross-attention module融合非增强与增强特征，mask decoder进行参数高效适配。

Lightweight method: 摘要仅提到mask decoder以"parameter-efficient manner"适配，但未具体说明剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、推理速度等部署信息。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。SAM本身参数量巨大（~600M+），不适用于星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：质子治疗中获取的肝脏病灶NCCT数据（具体规模未说明）；评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标名称（如Dice, IoU等）。

Experiments: 与U-Net和SAM-based方法对比，ViPSAM表现更优。摘要未提供消融实验、定量数值等详细信息。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 基于SAM的框架参数量大，不适合部署；3. 仅针对医学NCCT分割，领域迁移性差；4. 缺乏具体轻量化手段和部署指标。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴点有限。如果硬要提取：1）跨模态视觉提示（visual prompting）的思路可类比遥感中的多模态融合（如光学+SAR）；2）参数高效微调（parameter-efficient fine-tuning）的思路可用于遥感预训练模型在轨适配。但整体与课题方向弱相关。

Abstract: In proton therapy planning, respiratory-gated non-contrast CT (NCCT) is commonly used for lesion segmentation; however, accurate delineation remains challenging due to low lesion-to-background contrast. Although learning-based methods have shown strong performance, they often struggle with non-contrast image segmentation. Inspired by clinical practice, where contrast-enhanced MRI is referenced to delineate lesions on NCCT, we propose ViPSAM, a visual prompting framework that leverages complementary cross-modality information. Built upon the Segment Anything Model (SAM), ViPSAM introduces a visual prompt encoder to extract guidance features from contrast-enhanced images and a visual-guided cross-attention module to integrate non-contrast and contrast-enhanced features, thereby enhancing lesion-relevant representations in low-contrast regions. The mask decoder is further adapted in a parameter-efficient manner to utilize visual prompts effectively. We evaluate the proposed method on liver lesion segmentation using NCCT acquired for proton therapy. Experimental results demonstrate that ViPSAM outperforms representative U-Net- and SAM-based methods, indicating that cross-modality visual prompting enables more robust and accurate segmentation in non-contrast images.

</details>


### [145] [Domain Adaptation of Mismatched Proximal Denoiser for Plug-and-Play Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14894)
*Guixian Xu,Jinglai Li,Junqi Tang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出"近端失配"概念，分析PnP-PGD图像重建方法在去噪器域外部署时的收敛性，并提出基于近端匹配的自适应策略来改善域偏移下的重建质量。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于图像重建（deblurring, super-resolution）中的PnP-PGD算法收敛性分析，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或轻量化部署。虽然"域外部署"概念与在轨部署中的域偏移有一定类比价值，但核心问题、任务场景和方法论均不匹配。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：图像重建（Gaussian deblurring, super-resolution）；应用场景：通用图像处理；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: PnP-PGD（plug-and-play proximal gradient descent）框架，使用去噪器作为隐式先验。涉及两类去噪器族：learned proximal networks 和 gradient-step denoisers。

Lightweight method: 不涉及轻量化方法。论文未讨论剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或任何模型压缩/加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。论文不涉及星上/边缘部署可行性分析。

Datasets and metrics: 实验任务：Gaussian deblurring 和 super-resolution；域偏移设置：substantial domain shift；摘要未说明具体数据集名称和评价指标。

Experiments: 实验表明，在域偏移下，proximal matching adaptation 相比 MSE-based adaptation 显著提升重建质量，在few-shot场景下增益最大。摘要未提供定量对比结果。

Limitations: 1. 论文仅针对图像重建任务，不涉及目标检测；2. 不涉及遥感场景；3. 不涉及任何轻量化或部署优化；4. 实验仅在通用图像任务（deblurring, super-resolution）上验证，未在遥感数据上测试。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，但以下概念有间接启发：1. "域外部署"（domain mismatch）问题与遥感在轨场景中训练域（地面数据）与部署域（星上数据）之间的分布偏移有类比性，可关注在轨模型如何适应域偏移；2. "proximal matching"作为一种自适应策略，可类比思考在轨目标检测模型如何通过少量在轨数据微调来适应星上分布变化；3. 但本文方法本身（PnP-PGD图像重建）与目标检测轻量化无直接可借鉴的技术点。

Abstract: Plug-and-play proximal gradient descent (PnP-PGD) enables flexible image reconstruction by using denoisers as implicit priors. In practice, these denoisers are often deployed outside their training domains. Existing analyses establish convergence under structural assumptions on the deployed denoiser, such as requiring it to be a proximal map or a contraction. However, they do not measure how domain mismatch affects convergence of PnP-PGD. We define this effect as \emph{proximal mismatch}: the discrepancy between a deployed denoiser $\widehat{\mathsf D}$ and a target-domain reference map $\mathsf D_\star=\operatorname{prox}_{R_\star}$ associated with the underlying regularizer $R_\star$. Under this mismatch, each denoising update becomes an inexact proximal step for the target objective. We further derive a stationarity bound that decays at a rate of $\mathcal{O}(1/K)$, with an additive term proportional to the average squared proximal mismatch. This result motivates adaptation via proximal matching rather than MSE-based adaptation alone. We study this approach with two established denoiser families: learned proximal networks and gradient-step denoisers. Experiments on Gaussian deblurring and super-resolution under substantial domain shift show that proximal matching adaptation improves reconstruction quality significantly over MSE-based adaptation, yielding the largest numerical gains in the few-shot regime.

</details>


### [146] [Deep Scene-Driven Ordering of Hadamard Basis for Single-Pixel Spectral Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15045)
*Brayan Monroy,Hans Garcia,Henry Arguello,Jorge Bacca*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种场景驱动的Hadamard基序贯优化方法，用于改进单像素光谱成像中的深度光学编码设计，提升VIS和NIR光谱图像质量。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于光谱成像硬件层面的光学编码优化（Single-Pixel Imaging + Deep Optical Coding Design），不涉及遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：光谱图像重建（spectral image reconstruction）；场景：环境监测、精准农业；传感器类型：单像素成像（SPI）系统；目标类别：不涉及目标检测。

Model architecture: 基于Deep Optical Coding Design (DOCD)框架，引入场景驱动的Hadamard基序贯选择机制，端到端优化调制模式。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何模型轻量化方法（无剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 光谱数据集（spectral datasets）及真实测试台采集（real test-bed acquisitions）；评价指标：图像质量（具体指标摘要未说明）。

Experiments: 在仿真光谱数据集和真实测试台上验证，相比固定设计（fixed designs）提升了VIS和NIR光谱图像质量。未与目标检测方法对比。

Limitations: 1. 完全不涉及目标检测任务；2. 不涉及任何模型轻量化或压缩技术；3. 不涉及遥感卫星在轨场景；4. 方法本质是光学硬件层面的编码优化，而非深度学习模型优化。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。如果研究方向涉及光谱成像前端优化，可参考其场景驱动Hadamard基序贯选择思路，但与本课题"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"的核心目标（检测精度-速度-参数量权衡、星上部署）无直接关联。

Abstract: Spectral images are highly valuable for various applications, including environmental monitoring and precision agriculture. However, the high cost of specialized sensors limits the wide use of this technology in numerous applications. Current alternatives to acquire high spatial-spectral resolution spectral images, like Single-Pixel Imaging (SPI) enhanced with Deep Optical Coding Design (DOCD), have limitations due to their non-feedback optical designs, leading to limited image quality, with optimal performance achieved only for the specific scenes used during training. This work reformulates the DOCD framework to handle the scene-driven ordering of the Hadamard basis within the SPI architecture for spectral imaging. Taking into account that SPI usually acquires hundreds of snapshots, our approach introduces a scene-driven ordering of the Hadamard matrix for flexible SPI modulation pattern selection based on scene characteristics in an end-to-end optimization. Simulations on spectral datasets and real test-bed acquisitions demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method in improving the quality of VIS and NIR spectral images compared to fixed designs.

</details>


### [147] [ESAR: Event-Based Synthetic Aperture Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15073)
*Harbir Antil,Daniel Blauvelt,David Sayre*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出将事件相机成像建模为合成孔径逆问题，通过正则化反演恢复静态场景对数辐射场，在近天底方向事件数据上恢复大尺度空间结构。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦事件相机（event camera）的成像模型与重建，不涉及遥感卫星目标检测，也不涉及模型轻量化。虽然使用了"Falcon Neuro"近天底事件数据，但这是事件相机而非传统遥感光学/SAR传感器，且任务为场景重建而非目标检测。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"课题方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：事件相机成像重建（event-based imaging reconstruction）；场景：近天底（near-nadir）静态场景；传感器：事件相机（event camera）；目标类别：不涉及目标检测，无具体目标类别。

Model architecture: 提出基于线性化模型 APθ = b+η 的合成孔径逆问题框架，其中P为几何投影映射，A为时间差分算子，θ为静态场景对数辐射场。使用正则化反演（regularized inversion）恢复θ。未涉及神经网络或深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署可行性分析。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：模拟数据（simulated data）和真实Falcon Neuro近天底事件数据（real near-nadir Falcon Neuro event data）；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标，仅提及恢复大尺度空间结构（coherent large-scale spatial structure）和抑制细粒度纹理（suppressing fine-scale texture）。

Experiments: 与动态潜像（dynamic latent-image）和学习型事件重建基线（learned event-reconstruction baselines）对比，所提θ-based方法能恢复更连贯的大尺度空间结构。摘要未提供定量对比结果。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星目标检测完全无关，不涉及任何检测任务；2. 不涉及模型轻量化或部署；3. 方法基于线性化模型和正则化反演，计算复杂度可能较高，不适合实时在轨处理；4. 仅在近天底场景验证，泛化性未知；5. 恢复结果抑制细粒度纹理，不利于小目标检测。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义有限。唯一可能的启发点是：事件相机在近天底视角下的合成孔径建模思路，若未来考虑事件相机用于遥感运动目标检测，其稀疏异步特性可能对星上低功耗处理有潜在价值。但当前论文本身不涉及检测和轻量化，直接借鉴价值低。

Abstract: Event cameras report asynchronous polarity events when changes in log--radiance exceed a fixed contrast threshold, producing signed temporal contrast measurements rather than conventional image frames. We formulate monocular event-based imaging as a synthetic-aperture inverse problem for a static ground-domain log--radiance field $θ\in \mathbb{R}^{N_g}$. Instead of reconstructing a latent pixel-time volume $v \in \mathbb{R}^{N_pN_t}$, we impose the geometric relation $v=Pθ$, where $P$ maps the fixed scene into motion-dependent latent views. Aggregating events over finite time intervals gives the linearized model \[
  APθ= b+η, \] where $A$ is a temporal differencing operator, $b$ contains signed binned event counts, and $η$ represents measurement and modeling errors. This decomposition exposes a synthetic-aperture structure: under near-nadir motion, successive projections are approximately shifted views of a common scene, while the composite operator $AP$ remains ill-conditioned because it combines spatial averaging with temporal differencing. We therefore use regularized inversion to recover $θ$. Numerical experiments on simulated data and real near-nadir Falcon Neuro event data show that the proposed $θ$-based formulation recovers coherent large-scale spatial structure, relative to dynamic latent-image and learned event-reconstruction baselines, while suppressing fine-scale texture.

</details>

### [148] [Generalized Neural Distributional Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14122)
*Natan Hilario da Silva,Vicente Garibay Cancho,Adriano Kamimura Suzuki*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出GNDR框架，将深度神经网络嵌入经典概率分布参数空间，实现不确定性量化，适用于临床计数、生存分析、面部图像年龄分布等任务。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于概率分布回归与不确定性量化，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化中的任何一项。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议优先阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：概率分布回归与不确定性量化；应用场景：临床计数、转录组生存分析、面部图像年龄估计；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 将深度神经网络嵌入经典概率分布参数空间，上游网络作为固定非线性基展开，终端预测头隔离处理。
