Abstract: Approximate-message-passing methods offer fast Bayesian inference for high-dimensional inverse problems, but their performance and state-evolution predictions rely on correctly specified module parameters. This paper develops an adaptive version of score-based vector approximate message passing (SC-VAMP). Each parameterized factor is updated by a local tilted expectation-maximization (EM) step that reuses the tilted moments already computed by the single-input single-output module interface. Under standard large-system state-evolution assumptions and identifiability conditions, the matched parameters form a Bayes-optimal population fixed point of the adaptive recursion. The argument is written separately for prior modules and likelihood/LMMSE modules, the latter using the Gaussian cavity induced by the VAMP transformed-error model. Numerical results for linear and one-bit Bernoulli-Gaussian compressed sensing show that the proposed updates recover near-oracle performance from strongly mismatched initializations.

</details>


### [238] [Efficient Quantum Algorithm for Phase Optimization of 1-Bit RIS-Assisted MIMO Communication System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14700)
*Soumyadip Paul,Neel Kanth Kundu*

TL;DR: 提出一种基于量子近似优化算法（QAOA-LR）的1-bit RIS辅助MIMO通信系统相位优化方法，使用确定性线性斜坡调度替代经典优化器，在IBM量子处理器上验证了近ML性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是量子通信/量子计算在RIS辅助MIMO系统中的应用，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Model architecture: QAOA-LR（Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm with Linear Ramp），使用固定线性斜坡调度设置p层变分参数。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文中的"降低计算复杂度"是指量子算法相对经典优化的复杂度优势，与模型剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化方法无关。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。论文提到IBM量子处理器上的硬件实验，但这是量子计算硬件，与遥感卫星在轨部署的FPGA/NPU/TensorRT等无关。

Datasets and metrics: 蒙特卡洛仿真（Rayleigh衰落MIMO信道）；IBM量子处理器硬件实验；评价指标：与最优最大似然（ML）解的接近程度、容量性能、量子处理单元执行时间。

Experiments: 蒙特卡洛仿真表明QAOA-LR紧密跟踪最优ML解；IBM量子处理器硬件实验证实近ML容量性能，且量子处理单元执行时间随RIS元素数量呈多项式增长。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关，无任何可借鉴的检测或轻量化方法信息。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于量子通信优化领域，与遥感目标检测和模型轻量化没有交集。

Abstract: We propose a Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm with a deterministic linear ramp schedule (QAOA-LR) for phase optimization of a 1-bit RIS-assisted MIMO communication system. Each RIS element is restricted to a binary phase shift of 0 or π, turning the passive beamforming design problem with N elements into a combinatorial optimization problem over 2^N configurations. Instead of running a classical optimizer, QAOA-LR uses a fixed linear ramp to set the variational parameters across p layers and finds the best scale via a simple one-dimensional grid search over a single parameter. Monte Carlo simulations over Rayleigh-fading MIMO channels confirm that QAOA-LR closely tracks the optimum maximum-likelihood (ML) solution. Furthermore, the proposed algorithm reduces the computational complexity compared with classical optimization, and real hardware experiments on the IBM Quantum processor confirm near-ML capacity performance with polynomial scaling of quantum processing unit execution time as the number of RIS elements increases.

</details>


### [239] [Effect of Antenna Deployment on Achievable Rate in Cooperative Magnetic Induction Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14701)
*Honglei Ma,Erwu Liu,Rui Wang,Xinyu Qu*

Abstract: Magnetic Induction (MI) communication can be applied in some through-the-earth scenarios such as mines and underground rivers. To increase the transmission rate of MI communications, we propose a cooperative MI (CMI) scheme with an amplify-and-forward (AF) relay. Different from existing studies, we mainly focus on the relay with arbitrary antenna position and orientation (antenna deployment, AD). We derive the closed-form expression of CMI achievable data rate gain (CMG) for the relay and the closed-form expression of CMI channel bandwidth. Simulations reveal that a relay with appropriate AD could yield a significant increase in the achievable rate for MI systems.

</details>

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化基本无关。虽然摘要中提到了"satellite constellations"（卫星星座）作为应用场景之一，但论文核心是时间序列熵值分析方法，不涉及目标检测、模型压缩或星上部署等核心问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：时间序列群体复杂度分析（entropy estimation）；应用场景：物理科学、生命科学、环境科学中的轨迹集合分析（包括卫星星座轨迹、混沌流、临床队列等）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。方法核心是CWMMSE（聚类加权多变量多尺度样本熵），包含聚类分组和加权熵计算两个步骤。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：11个物理、环境、工程和生物医学系统数据集（具体名称摘要未说明）；评价指标：CWMMSE熵值、强相合性（strong consistency）。

Experiments: 在11个系统上验证，包括：平静海洋区域排名高于能量更高但个体更复杂的区域；识别大地震为系统复杂度崩塌；在心脏队列中逆转了平均方法的结论（疾病降低群体多样性）。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测和轻量化完全无关；2. 方法基于固定有限划分（fixed finite partition），未讨论划分粒度选择问题；3. 摘要未说明计算复杂度或大规模数据可扩展性。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能的弱关联是：如果未来研究涉及卫星星座轨迹数据的在轨分析（而非目标检测），CWMMSE的群体复杂度概念可能有参考价值，但与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题方向不匹配。

Abstract: Across the physical and life sciences, data increasingly appear as ensembles of trajectories, from chaotic flows and satellite constellations to clinical cohorts. Established sample-entropy measures characterize individual time series, while averaging across an ensemble discards population structure and cannot distinguish redundancy from diversity. We introduce clustering-weighted multivariate multiscale sample entropy (CWMMSE), which groups trajectories into behavioral patterns and weights each by its dynamical complexity. CWMMSE is a weighted entropy of the population's pattern distribution. Its empirical plug-in estimator is strongly consistent for a fixed finite partition, and it separates two components that can diverge in real data: individual complexity and population diversity. Both are essential. Averaging ignores diversity, whereas spread alone can mistake a varied but predictable population for a complex one. Across eleven physical, environmental, engineering, and biomedical systems, CWMMSE ranks a calm ocean region above an energetic but individually more complex one, identifies a major earthquake as a collapse in system complexity, and reverses the conclusion from averaging in cardiac cohorts, where disease reduces population diversity. Supported by an open, reproducible implementation, these results show that population complexity should be measured rather than averaged.

</details>


### [241] [Elliptic Range-Doppler Mapping for OFDM-ISAC under IQ Imbalance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14775)
*Thrassos K. Oikonomou,Dimitrios Bozanis,Sotiris A. Tegos,Panagiotis D. Diamantoulakis,George K. Karagiannidis*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对OFDM-ISAC系统中接收机IQ不平衡问题，提出一种椭圆组正交匹配追踪检测器，直接在受IQI污染的观测上进行稀疏恢复，无需先补偿IQI再处理。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是OFDM-ISAC（通感一体化）系统中的IQI补偿与目标检测问题，属于通信/雷达感知领域，不涉及遥感卫星、光学/SAR图像、目标检测模型或轻量化部署。

Reading priority: 1分。论文研究OFDM-ISAC系统中的IQI补偿问题，属于通信/雷达感知领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关，不建议深入阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：OFDM-ISAC系统中的目标检测（稀疏恢复）；场景：通感一体化雷达感知；传感器类型：通信雷达波形（OFDM）；目标类别：物理目标（延迟-多普勒域）。不涉及遥感图像、卫星、光学/SAR/高光谱传感器。

Model architecture: 提出椭圆组正交匹配追踪（Elliptic Group OMP）检测器，基于椭圆原子组表示，通过两次加权2D-FFT和局部组投影实现高效相关计算。非深度学习模型，不涉及CNN/Transformer等视觉模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明轻量化方法。方法本身通过FFT加速计算，但并非针对模型压缩（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）设计。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。仅提及通过两次加权2D-FFT实现高效计算，但未给出具体数值或与星上/边缘部署相关的讨论。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：未说明具体数据集，使用数值仿真（Numerical results）；评价指标：精确支持恢复（exact support recovery）、弱目标检测性能（weak-target detection）。

Experiments: 与对应基准方法对比，在中度和强IQI条件下，所提方法在精确支持恢复和弱目标检测方面表现更优。摘要未给出具体数值对比。

Limitations: 1. 仅针对OFDM-ISAC场景，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测无直接关联；2. 未涉及深度学习模型，无法为轻量化网络设计提供直接借鉴；3. 仅在数值仿真中验证，缺乏真实遥感数据实验；4. 未讨论星上部署的硬件约束（功耗、算力、存储）。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。可能的间接启发：1. 稀疏恢复思想（OMP类方法）可用于遥感目标检测中的特征选择或模型剪枝；2. 利用FFT加速计算的思想可用于轻量化检测器的算子优化；3. 对噪声/失配鲁棒性的处理思路可迁移到遥感在轨场景中的非理想条件（如辐射畸变、几何形变）。但整体而言，该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向关联度极低。

Abstract: Receiver in-phase/quadrature imbalance (IQI) couples each OFDM subcarrier with its mirror counterpart, creating ghost targets and degrading range-Doppler recovery in orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) integrated sensing and communication (ISAC). Instead of first compensating for IQI and then applying conventional processing, this letter exploits the structure of the IQI-impaired observation directly. We show that each physical target induces coupled direct and mirror components linked through the target coefficient and its conjugate, which motivates an elliptic atom group representation for each candidate delay-Doppler cell. Based on this model, we propose an elliptic group orthogonal matching pursuit detector that performs sparse recovery directly on the received OFDM grid. The required correlations are computed efficiently through two weighted two-dimensional fast Fourier transforms (FFTs) followed by local group projections. Numerical results show that the proposed method improves exact support recovery and weak-target detection compared to corresponding benchmarks, especially under moderate and strong receiver IQI.

</details>


### [242] [Conditional Generative Learning Enabled Wireless UAV Sensing and Tracking via Point Cloud Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14778)
*Xinhong Dai,Yuan Gao,Hao Jiang,Xiaojun Yuan,Xin Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于阵列天线的无人机感知与跟踪方法AUGUST，利用电磁点云成像重建无人机位置、姿态和形状信息。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是地面基站对无人机的感知与跟踪（电磁点云成像），不涉及遥感卫星、不涉及目标检测、不涉及模型轻量化。虽然涉及无人机（UAV），但场景是地面基站感知而非遥感卫星对地观测。

Model architecture: AUGUST方法包含两个模块：(1) conditional channel encoding module（条件通道编码模块），含position embedding和SNR embedding；(2) generative decoding module（生成式解码模块），基于diffusion model（扩散模型）和weighted training objective。整体为生成式模型，非检测模型。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。该方法基于扩散模型，计算开销通常较大，不适合星上部署。

Datasets and metrics: 使用仿真数据（simulation results），未说明具体数据集名称。评价指标：点云重建 fidelity（保真度）、UAV姿态和形状信息捕获精度、定位性能。未使用遥感目标检测常用指标如mAP、F1等。

Experiments: 与benchmark schemes对比，AUGUST方法在点云重建保真度上更高，能更准确捕获无人机姿态和形状信息；相比传统基于模型的方法在定位性能上有显著提升。未与任何目标检测方法对比。

Limitations: (1) 仅基于仿真数据验证，缺乏真实场景实验；(2) 扩散模型推理速度慢，计算开销大；(3) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；(4) 未讨论模型轻量化或实时部署问题。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能的间接启发：论文中position embedding和SNR embedding的编码思路，在遥感卫星目标检测中处理不同信噪比或不同轨道高度下的特征稳定性时可能有参考价值，但关联性很弱。

Abstract: In this paper, we study an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) sensing and tracking problem, where a base station equipped with an antenna array continuously illuminates a flying UAV and exploits the reflected echoes for slot-wise point cloud imaging within its potential flight region. To accomplish this task, the imaging region for each slot is determined based on the prior of the historical UAV positions. Then, the UAV is represented by an electromagnetic point cloud in this region that contains its spatial information and electromagnetic properties (EPs), enabling the unified extraction of UAV position, attitude, and shape from the reconstructed point cloud. The EP point cloud imaging for the UAV based on echo signals is a complex inverse problem. To this end, we propose an Array-based Unified Generative UAV Sensing and Tracking (AUGUST) approach, which integrates a conditional channel encoding module and a generative decoding module. The encoding module incorporates position and signal-to-noise ratio embeddings to stabilize the UAV intrinsic feature extraction under fast UAV position and channel variations, and maps the encoded features to a latent space regularized by a learnable flow-based prior. The decoding module employs a diffusion model with a weighted training objective to reconstruct the UAV point cloud guided by the extracted features. The simulation results demonstrate that the reconstructed point clouds via the proposed AUGUST approach present higher fidelity compared to the benchmark schemes, thereby enabling a more accurate capture of the UAV attitude and shape information. The AUGUST approach also presents a substantial gain over the conventional model-based baseline in positioning performance.

</details>


### [243] [Jacobi Elliptic Chirps for Sub-Nyquist Multi-Target Ranging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14783)
*Dimitrios Bozanis,Thrassos K. Oikonomou,Sotiris A. Tegos,Panagiotis D. Diamantoulakis,George K. Karagiannidis*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种正弦-余弦雅可比椭圆调频波形(SC-EFM)，用于解决亚奈奎斯特采样脉冲压缩雷达中鬼影目标检测与目标分辨力之间的权衡问题。

Experiments: 数值结果表明：SC-EFM相比LFM显著抑制鬼影检测，同时保持与LFM相当的目标分辨力；相比HFM在分辨邻近目标方面表现更优。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化完全无关；2. 仅涉及雷达信号波形设计，不涉及视觉/图像目标检测；3. 未讨论任何深度学习或模型压缩方法。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于雷达信号处理领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化方向无交叉。

Abstract: Sub-Nyquist sampling is an attractive way to reduce the hardware cost of wideband pulse-compression radar, but it introduces coherent alias-induced replicas in the matched-filter range profile, producing spurious peaks known as ghost targets. Existing frequency-modulated waveforms face a practical trade-off in this regime: linear frequency-modulated (LFM) pulses provide compact range responses but are highly susceptible to ghost-target detections, whereas hyperbolic frequency-modulated (HFM) pulses suppress ghosts at the cost of degraded target separability. To overcome this trade-off, we propose a sine-over-cosine Jacobi elliptic frequency-modulated waveform, referred to as SC-EFM, in which the elliptic modulus tunes the instantaneous-frequency (IF) curvature while preserving the pulse duration and bandwidth of conventional benchmarks. We characterize the sub-Nyquist folding structure of SC-EFM and derive closed-form expressions for the multi-target and ghost-target detection probabilities. Numerical results show that SC-EFM significantly suppresses ghost detections relative to LFM while matching its target separability, and substantially outperforms HFM in resolving close targets, providing a unified waveform solution for ghost-resilient sub-Nyquist multi-target ranging.

</details>


### [244] [LIVE-RIS: Real-Time In-Flight Actuation of UAV-Mounted RIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14851)
*David Müller,Kevin Weinberger,Aydin Sezgin,Martin Mönnigmann*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个搭载于无人机上的可重构智能表面（RIS）实时原型系统，通过无人机扩展卡尔曼滤波和机载传感器预测RIS姿态，实现实时重配置以抑制扰动影响，并通过真实实验验证性能。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文主题是6G无线通信中的UAV-mounted RIS原型验证，完全不涉及遥感图像、卫星在轨处理、目标检测或模型轻量化。虽然提到了UAV（无人机），但UAV在此仅作为RIS的搭载平台用于通信链路增强，而非遥感观测平台。

Model architecture: 核心系统为UAV-mounted RIS原型，包含RIS硬件、UAV平台、扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）用于姿态预测。不涉及任何深度学习模型或目标检测网络架构。

Experiments: 通过实验测量验证了UAV-mounted RIS在真实扰动和硬件约束下的性能，评估了不同部署位置的影响。未涉及目标检测实验或模型对比。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。论文本身局限包括：仅针对特定UAV-RIS场景，未讨论大规模部署或与其他通信技术的融合。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。若强行关联，UAV-mounted RIS中的实时姿态估计与补偿思路（EKF+机载传感器）在遥感卫星平台中也有类似应用（如卫星姿态抖动补偿），但这与目标检测模型轻量化无关。

Abstract: Reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) are emerging as a key technology for sixth-generation (6G) wireless networks due to their ability to dynamically control the propagation environment. To ensure favorable Line-of-Sight (LoS) conditions in real-world applications, the RIS is mounted on an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV). While the potential of UAV-mounted RIS has been extensively studied in theoretical works, experimental validation with real-world data remains limited. Such validation is particularly important, as UAV motion and disturbances may degrade the performance of the RIS-enabled link. In this paper, we present the first fully functional, real-time capable UAV-mounted RIS prototype and validate its performance through experimental measurements under realistic disturbances and hardware constraints. We show that the RIS pose can be predicted based on the UAV's extended Kalman filter (EKF) and onboard sensors. By utilizing this estimation, we demonstrate that the RIS can be reconfigured in real time, effectively mitigating disturbance effects and preserving the performance gains of the RIS-enabled link. Furthermore, we systematically evaluate different deployment locations to provide insights into RIS performance in real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [245] [Learning-Driven Channel Representation for Wireless Localization: From Channel Observations to Location Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14938)

Limitations: 论文指出从实验研究到实际部署仍存在关键挑战（key challenges），包括信道表征的质量和可用性对定位性能起决定性作用，且有用信息与环境布局、时间动态、硬件差异和系统配置紧密耦合，导致跨场景性能一致性差。

Ideas for my research: 与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无可借鉴点。该论文属于无线通信定位领域，不涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型压缩加速技术。建议忽略此论文。

Abstract: Wireless observations capture radio signal responses formed through interactions with propagation environments and spatial geometry. In integrated sensing and communication, such observations have become an important basis for high-accuracy localization beyond conventional channel estimation. Learning-driven methods learn implicit relations between channel propagation and spatial position, enabling location inference under complex channel conditions. However, the useful information is tightly coupled with environmental layout, temporal dynamics, hardware differences, and system configurations. This coupling obscures the inference process and weakens performance consistency across scenarios. In this paper, we model the localization process as a unified ``wireless observation--channel representation--location inference'' framework, and review learning-driven high-accuracy localization techniques with channel representations as the organizing view. The survey covers typical channel observation forms and analyzes their physical meanings. We also review channel feature extraction and representation learning methods, and summarize methods according to the acquisition, organization, adaptation, and reuse of channel representations. Typical methods are compared in terms of accuracy, applicable conditions, data requirements, and generalization. We highlight that the quality and usability of channel representations are critical to exploiting propagation information, and thus play a decisive role in localization performance. Finally, we summarize the key challenges in moving from experimental studies to real deployment and present our perspectives on these issues.

</details>


### [246] [Achievable-Rate Analysis of MISO Systems with Transmit-Side Multiport Matching Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14992)
*Wendong Cheng,Li Chen,Weidong Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 研究MISO系统中多端口匹配网络（MMN）在Bode-Fano约束下的可达速率上界及电路实现方法，属于通信电路与信息论交叉领域。

Research relevance: 1分。与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于射频前端电路的多端口匹配网络（MMN）在MISO通信系统中的可达速率理论分析与电路实现，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化任何维度。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文涉及多端口匹配网络（MMN）的电路结构，但这是射频电路设计，非深度学习模型。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署指标。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于通信电路与信息论领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交叉。

Abstract: Characterizing communication performance under the physical constraints imposed by radio frequency front-end circuits is essential for bridging communication-theoretic analysis and practical circuit design. In this work, we investigate the achievable-rate upper bound of a multiple-input single-output (MISO) system with a transmitter-side interconnected multiport matching network (MMN) under multiport Bode--Fano constraints and develop a rate-oriented MMN circuit realization method. First, based on a circuit-theoretic communication model and the concept of directional gain from multivariable control theory, we reveal how the directional characteristics of MMN power transmission interact with wireless propagation to affect communication performance. Then, building on this directional-gain interpretation, we reformulate the matrix-valued functional optimization problem that characterizes the achievable-rate upper bound and derive the optimal transmission-coefficient structure. Further, a greedy constraint-wise repair method is developed to obtain a feasible suboptimal solution. Finally, through a rate-oriented MMN circuit realization method, we demonstrate that the derived theoretical insights provide effective guidance for practical MMN design. Numerical results validate the theoretical analysis of the achievable-rate upper bound and the effectiveness of the proposed MMN circuit realization method.

</details>


### [247] [On-board AI-based Channel Estimation for LEO NTNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15127)
*Mahdi Abdollahpour,Bruno De Filippo,Carla Amatetti,Alessandro Vanelli-Coralli*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种面向非地面网络(NTN)星上实时推理的轻量化卷积信道估计器，相比SOTA AI模型参数效率提升27%，计算量降低约29倍，同时MSE性能更优。

Research relevance: 2分——弱相关。论文涉及卫星(NTN)和星上(onboard)推理，但任务为信道估计(channel estimation)，而非遥感目标检测。轻量化方法(卷积设计、参数效率、FLOPs降低)对轻量化思路有参考价值，但场景和任务完全不同。

Reading priority: 2分——弱相关。通信信道估计与遥感目标检测任务差异大，但轻量化星上部署的思路有一定启发。不建议作为核心参考文献，可快速浏览轻量化设计思路。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：信道估计(channel estimation)；场景：非地面网络(NTN)、低轨卫星通信、星上实时推理；传感器/数据类型：通信信号(非遥感图像)；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 轻量化卷积神经网络(lightweight convolution-based estimator)，具体结构细节摘要未说明。利用领域知识(domain knowledge)进行参数效率优化。

Lightweight method: 轻量化卷积设计、参数效率优化(parameter efficiency)、利用领域知识减少参数冗余。相比SOTA AI模型参数效率提升27%，相比传统方法FLOPs减少约29倍。未涉及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等经典轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 面向星上实时推理(onboard inference)，在NGSO功率预算约束下评估。参数量：摘要未说明具体数值；FLOPs：相比传统方法减少约29倍；延迟：摘要未说明；显存：摘要未说明；能耗：在功率受限星上节点(power-constrained spaceborne nodes)下评估，但具体能耗数值未说明。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：摘要未说明(可能为仿真信道数据)；评价指标：MSE(Mean Squared Error)、端到端链路性能(end-to-end link performance)。

Experiments: 与已有算法(established algorithms)和SOTA AI模型对比，在MSE指标上表现更优，参数效率提升27%，FLOPs减少约29倍。具体对比方法和消融实验摘要未说明。

Limitations: 1. 任务为信道估计，与遥感目标检测完全无关；2. 未涉及遥感图像处理、目标检测、旋转框检测等核心任务；3. 轻量化方法仅提及利用领域知识，未给出具体网络结构或可复现细节；4. 未在真实遥感/卫星硬件上部署验证。

Ideas for my research: 1. 利用领域知识(domain knowledge)指导轻量化设计的思想可借鉴到遥感目标检测中(如利用遥感图像先验知识设计更高效的检测头或特征提取模块)；2. 参数效率提升和FLOPs大幅降低的思路对星上部署有参考价值；3. 但该论文方法本身无法直接迁移到遥感目标检测任务，需重新设计。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence(AI) methods have shown strong channel estimation performance in terrestrial networks, but they typically rely on substantial computational resources. As 6G moves toward a unified architecture that will include Non-Terrestrial Networks (NTN) from day 0, availability of large and power hungry computational resources shall not be taken for granted. At the same time, NTN propagation often exhibits high predictability, limited multipath richness and significant Doppler shifts, representing a specific channel estimation problem. In this work, we propose a lightweight convolution-based channel estimator designed specifically for NTN operation and real-time onboard inference. We evaluate its channel estimation accuracy under stringent NGSO power budgets and quantify the resulting end-to-end impact on link performance. We show the improvement in terms of Mean Squared Error (MSE) achieved by the proposed approach compared with established algorithms, demonstrating that efficient AI models can deliver robust performance even on power-constrained spaceborne nodes. In addition, the proposed design by exploiting the domain knowledge, improves parameter efficiency by $27\%$ compared with state-of-the-art AI models and requires approximately $29\times$ fewer floating-point operations than conventional methods while achieving superior MSE performance.

</details>


### [248] [Modular-CAPA-Based Communication Systems: Joint Activation and Beamforming Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15148)
*Mengyu Qian,Xidong Mu,Li You,Michail Matthaiou*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究基于模块化连续孔径阵列（CAPA）的多用户通信系统中的子孔径激活与波束赋形联合优化问题，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。本文研究的是无线通信领域的模块化CAPA多用户通信系统，涉及子孔径激活、波束赋形、频谱效率优化等问题。完全不涉及遥感、卫星观测、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。本文为无线通信领域的论文，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。本文任务为多用户通信中的子孔径激活与波束赋形设计，场景为无线通信系统，非遥感/卫星场景。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。本文涉及模块化CAPA（连续孔径阵列）架构，但这是通信天线阵列概念，非深度学习模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。本文提出的低复杂度空间带宽感知子孔径激活方案属于通信资源分配优化中的复杂度降低方法，与模型剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等轻量化技术无关。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。本文未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。本文使用数值仿真验证，未使用遥感或目标检测相关数据集。评价指标为频谱效率（SE）。

Experiments: 数值结果表明：1）部分激活的模块化CAPA架构性能优于相邻CAPA激活方案；2）提出的B&B算法在SE上优于所有基准方案；3）提出的空间带宽感知方案在性能-复杂度权衡上具有吸引力。

Limitations: 本文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化研究方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。

Ideas for my research: 本文与课题方向基本无关，无可借鉴点。但其中B&B（分支定界）算法处理混合整数优化问题的思路，在理论上可迁移至其他优化问题，不过与遥感目标检测模型轻量化的核心任务无直接关联。

Abstract: A modular continuous aperture array (CAPA)-based multi-user communication system is investigated, where only a portion of the aperture, namely sub-CAPAs, is activated to serve users. The signal model for the proposed modular CAPA is first introduced. Based on this model, a spectral efficiency (SE) maximization problem is formulated to jointly optimize the sub-CAPA activation and beamforming, subject to constraints on the limited number of active sub-CAPAs and the total transmit power. To address the resulting mixed-integer optimization problem, a branch-and-bound (B&B)-based algorithm is first proposed for optimal sub-CAPA activation and beamforming design. After that, the spatial bandwidth of the modular CAPA under partial activation is analyzed. The analysis reveals that a modular CAPA with partial sub-CAPAs activated could achieve a maximum spatial bandwidth comparable to that of a conventional CAPA. Motivated by this insight, a low-complexity spatial bandwidth-aware sub-CAPA activation scheme is further proposed. Finally, numerical results demonstrate that i) modular CAPA architectures with partial activation can consistently achieve greater performance gains than adjacent CAPA activations; ii) the proposed B&B scheme outperforms all benchmark schemes in terms of SE; and iii) the proposed spatial bandwidth-aware scheme provides an attractive performance-complexity tradeoff compared with the proposed B&B-based algorithm.

</details>


### [249] [Fast-Fading Channel and Power Optimization of the Magnetic Inductive Cellular Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.04737)
*Honglei Ma,Erwu Liu,Zhijun Fang,Rui Wang,Yongbin Gao,Wenjun Yu,Dongming Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出车辆磁感应（VMI）通信中天线振动导致的快衰落信道三维模型，推导其统计分布，并基于非合作博弈和多智能体Q-learning设计功率控制算法以优化网络吞吐量。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文研究的是地下磁感应通信中的信道建模与功率控制问题，属于无线通信物理层领域，与遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未涉及遥感或目标检测任务。应用场景为地下磁感应通信网络，传感器类型为磁感应天线，目标类别不适用。

Model architecture: 摘要未涉及深度学习模型或神经网络结构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未涉及星上/边缘部署相关信息，无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明使用的数据集。评价指标为CDF、PDF、期望、中断概率、网络吞吐量等通信性能指标。

Limitations: 该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接或间接的借鉴。

Abstract: The cellular network of magnetic Induction (MI) communication holds promise in long-distance underground environments. In the traditional MI communication, there is no fast-fading channel since the MI channel is treated as a quasi-static channel. However, for the vehicle (mobile) MI (VMI) communication, the unpredictable antenna vibration brings the remarkable fast-fading. As such fast-fading cannot be modeled by the central limit theorem, it differs radically from other wireless fast-fading channels. Unfortunately, few studies focus on this phenomenon. In this paper, using a novel space modeling based on the electromagnetic field theorem, we propose a 3-dimension model of the VMI antenna vibration. By proposing ``conjugate pseudo-piecewise functions'' and boundary $p(x)$ distribution, we derive the cumulative distribution function (CDF), probability density function (PDF) and the expectation of the VMI fast-fading channel. We also theoretically analyze the effects of the VMI fast-fading on the network throughput, including the VMI outage probability which can be ignored in the traditional MI channel study. We draw several intriguing conclusions different from those in wireless fast-fading studies. For instance, the fast-fading brings more uniformly distributed channel coefficients. Finally, we propose the power control algorithm using the non-cooperative game and multiagent Q-learning methods to optimize the throughput of the cellular VMI network. Simulations validate the derivation and the proposed algorithm.

</details>

### [250] [Intelligent Three Level Learning Architecture for Autonomous UAV Swarms in Search and Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14093)
*Oleksii Bychkov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种面向自主无人机蜂群搜救任务的三级分层学习架构，整合Hebbian神经可塑性、多智能体强化学习与元学习，通过形式化契约保证安全性等六类性质。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于无人机蜂群（UAV swarms）的搜救（search and rescue）任务，不涉及遥感卫星、Earth observation、aerial image目标检测，也不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。虽然无人机与遥感有一定交集，但论文核心是自主智能体协同与分层学习架构的形式化验证，而非遥感图像目标检测或星上部署。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：无人机蜂群自主搜救（search and rescue）；场景：非遥感卫星场景；传感器类型：摘要未说明；目标类别：摘要未说明（非检测任务）。

Model architecture: 三级分层学习架构：底层（reflexes）Hebbian神经可塑性用于个体适应；中层（skills）多智能体强化学习（MARL）+图神经网络（GNN）+行为树（Behavior Trees）用于战术协调；顶层（reasoning）模型无关元学习（MAML）+BDI推理+数字孪生用于战略决策。通过22个架构契约组织在6个组件中，提供6类形式化保证。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。无剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络设计等内容。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关指标。论文关注的是形式化验证和理论保证，而非实际部署效率。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明使用的数据集和评价指标。论文为理论性工作，未提及实验数据集。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果和对比实验。仅提供理论分析，证明该架构解决了现有分层RL的五项基本局限，并给出了集成定理和形式化保证。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 不涉及任何模型轻量化或边缘部署技术；3. 纯理论/形式化工作，缺乏实际实验验证；4. 架构复杂度高，实际工程落地难度大。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义极低。唯一可能间接参考的点：1）分层架构思想（高层策略/低层执行）在星上轻量化检测中可类比为"星上粗检测+地面精检测"的分级处理模式；2）形式化契约保证的思路可用于验证轻量化模型在星上的可靠性边界。但整体而言，与本课题方向关联度极低。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel three level hierarchical learning architecture for autonomous UAV swarms performing search and rescue operations. Unlike conventional approaches that apply a single learning paradigm across all hierarchy levels, the proposed architecture integrates three qualitatively different learning mechanisms corresponding to the biological hierarchy of reflexes, skills, and reasoning such as Hebbian neuroplasticity for individual agent adaptation, multi agent reinforcement learning with graph neural networks and behavior trees for tactical coordination, and model agnostic meta learning with BDI reasoning and a digital twin for strategic decision making.
  The architecture is formalized through twenty two architectural contracts organized across six components such as BDI, Behavior Trees, GNN, MARL, Neuroplasticity, Meta Learning that collectively provide six classes of formal guarantees such as safety, budget correctness, optimality, liveness, starvation freedom, and inter level consistency.
  We introduce Swarm Meta Cognition as a compositional property arising from the structured interaction of all three levels, enabling the swarm to monitor its own cognitive state and switch between cognitive strategies. Five constructive progress functions for SAR task types bridge the gap between abstract optimization theory and concrete operational scenarios.
  The main integration theorem establishes that when all contracts are satisfied, the hybrid neuro-symbolic system preserves all six guarantee classes. For the dynamic case with active learning, five new contracts extend the framework with three additional guarantees such as cognitive resilience, graceful degradation, and monotonic meta improvement. Theoretical analysis demonstrates that the architecture addresses five fundamental limitations of existing hierarchical RL approaches.

</details>


### [251] [HG-RAG: Hierarchy-Guided Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Structured Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14095)
*Pranav Yadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HG-RAG框架，通过层级知识图谱的图遍历检索为LLM提供结构化上下文，提升层次化、关系型和多跳推理能力。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于大语言模型(LLM)的检索增强生成(RAG)技术，属于自然语言处理/知识图谱领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：检索增强生成（RAG）中的结构化上下文检索；应用场景：LLM问答、层次化/关系型推理；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: HG-RAG框架：基于层级知识图谱的图遍历检索管道，包含命名实体锚定、向上（父节点）、横向（关系邻居）、向下（子节点）的上下文扩展机制。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 评估数据集：三个不同规模（18-800节点）的世界知识图谱；查询类型：local fact（局部事实）、hierarchical（层次化）、neighborhood（邻域）、multi-hop（多跳）；对比基线：dense retrieval baseline（密集检索基线）；指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标名称，提及了推理性能、幻觉减少、局部一致性保持。

Experiments: HG-RAG在层次化、关系型和多跳推理任务上持续优于扁平基线（flat baseline），同时减少幻觉并保持局部一致性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供该方向上的局限性分析。摘要未说明在更大规模知识图谱上的扩展性、检索延迟等。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于NLP/知识图谱领域，与遥感目标检测轻量化无技术交叉。如果强行关联，层级知识图谱的"由粗到细"检索思想或许可类比遥感检测中的多尺度特征融合或层级化检测头设计，但关联非常间接。

Abstract: Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) has proven to be a widely successful process at improving the quality of outputs from a Large Language Model (LLM) for wider context. However, RAG systems typically retrieve context from flat document stores, which struggles when queries require hierarchical or relational reasoning across structured knowledge. I present HG-RAG (Hierarchy-Guided RAG), a framework that performs graph-traversal over a hierarchical knowledge graph to deliver structured context to a language model. My retrieval pipeline resolves a named entity anchor from the query, then expands context upward through parent nodes, laterally through relational neighbors, and downward through child nodes when needed. I evaluate HG-RAG against a dense retrieval baseline across three world scales (18-800 nodes) with four query types: local fact, hierarchical, neighborhood, and multi-hop. Results show HG-RAG consistently outperforms the flat baseline on hierarchical, relational, and multi-hop reasoning tasks, while reducing hallucination and maintaining locality coherence.

</details>


### [252] [IMEX Interaction-Based Model Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14096)
*Emiliano Massi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为IMEX的交互式模型解释方法，用于识别预测建模中对目标预测贡献最大的变量及其交互作用，并在合成数据集上验证了其静态相关性度量PCS的有效性。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文属于可解释机器学习（XAI）领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。既没有遥感/卫星场景，也没有目标检测任务，更没有轻量化/部署相关内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文面向通用预测建模中的可解释性任务，不涉及遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: IMEX框架，包含两个互补度量：Static Correlation Power (PCS) 和 Interaction Correlation Power (PCI)。PCS量化单个特征的贡献，PCI捕捉特征间的非加性交互效应。方法不限制高阶交互分析（>2阶）。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何模型压缩或加速方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 三个具有已知结构的合成数据集（synthetic datasets with known structures）。对比方法为INVASE。评价指标未明确说明，通过特征级结构恢复能力进行验证。

Experiments: 在三个合成数据集上，将IMEX的PCS组件与INVASE进行对比实验，结果表明IMEX能够在输入特征与预测目标之间存在非线性、条件性和多重共线性关系时，恢复相关的特征级结构。

Limitations: 1. 仅在合成数据集上验证，缺乏真实遥感/视觉任务上的实验；2. 仅验证了PCS组件，PCI组件未进行实验验证；3. 与遥感在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题基本无直接可借鉴点。唯一的间接启发是：在模型压缩/剪枝过程中，可解释性方法（如特征重要性分析）可能辅助判断哪些通道或层对检测任务更重要，从而指导结构化剪枝。但本论文的IMEX方法面向表格数据而非视觉特征，迁移到遥感目标检测场景需要大量改造。

Abstract: In predictive modeling, the ability to explain why a model produces a given target prediction has become increasingly important [5, 10]. Black-box models do not provide a transparent description of the internal mechanisms that generate the prediction, making even accurate predictions difficult to interpret and validate. In critical contexts, predictive accuracy alone is not a sufficient validation metric if the reasons underlying model decisions remain unexplained. The IMEX (Interaction-Based Model Explanation) approach represents a methodological direction within explainable predictive modeling. IMEX is designed to identify which variables contribute most to the target prediction and which interactions among variables are significant in determining the target. The method does not impose limitations on higher-order interaction analysis, allowing the investigation of feature subsets with cardinality greater than two. Beyond the identification of feature importance, IMEX enables the exploration of interaction patterns that may be consistent with latent mechanisms influencing the outcome. Through the application of the IMEX algorithm, it is possible to construct an interpretability map of the predictions. The IMEX framework is built on two complementary metrics: Static Correlation Power (PCS), which quantifies the contribution of individual features, and Interaction Correlation Power (PCI), which captures non-additive effects among features. In the present work, the PCS component is experimentally validated through a comparison with INVASE [18] on three synthetic datasets with known structures. The results indicate that IMEX can recover relevant feature-level structures in the presence of non-linear, conditional, and multicollinear relationships between input features and prediction targets.

</details>


### [253] [RegNetAgents: A Multi-Agent Framework for Cross-Network Regulatory Driver Identification in Cancer Genomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14097)
*Jose A. Bird*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个面向基因调控网络的多智能体框架RegNetAgents，用于癌症基因组学中的调控候选基因识别，与遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分（基本无关）。该论文研究的是生物信息学/癌症基因组学领域的基因调控网络分析，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：基因调控网络中的调控候选基因识别（regulatory candidate identification）；应用场景：癌症基因组学（breast cancer, colorectal cancer）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 多智能体框架（multi-agent framework），基于LangGraph DAG workflow实现，包含dual-network classification、cancer gene filtering、mode-of-action assignment等模块。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署，未报告参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：TCGA-derived cancer networks、GREMln项目的大规模单细胞调控网络、OncoKB注释；评价指标：Stouffer Z-score、p-value、富集分析（enrichment analysis）；实验设置：11个乳腺癌（BRCA）和12个结直肠癌（COAD）focal genes。

Experiments: TCGA来源候选基因显著富集（BRCA: Z=6.69, COAD: Z=6.95）；GREMln来源候选基因也显著富集（BRCA: Z=5.51, COAD: Z=7.06, all p<0.0001）；管家基因和非驱动对照基因集无富集，支持信号特异性。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于生物信息学/计算基因组学领域，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: We introduce RegNetAgents, an AI-oriented multi-agent framework for structured, query-driven regulatory candidate identification across heterogeneous gene regulatory networks. The system enables unified analysis of bulk tumor and single-cell-derived ARACNe networks by integrating TCGA-derived cancer networks with large-scale single-cell regulatory networks from the GREmLN project. For a given focal gene, the framework performs dual-network classification, cancer gene filtering using OncoKB annotations, and mode-of-action (MoA) assignment for tumor-derived regulatory relationships. Candidates are ranked by evidence consistency across networks (Both, TCGA-only, GREmLN-only). The system is implemented as a multi-agent LangGraph DAG workflow, accessible through a unified Python API and Model Context Protocol (MCP) client, operating as a downstream analytical layer over precomputed regulatory networks rather than a network inference method. Across eleven breast cancer (BRCA) and twelve colorectal cancer (COAD) focal genes, RegNetAgents identifies candidate regulators significantly enriched for OncoKB-annotated cancer genes. TCGA-derived candidates show strong enrichment (Stouffer Z = 6.69 for BRCA and 6.95 for COAD), while GREmLN-derived candidates also demonstrate significant enrichment (Z = 5.51 for BRCA and 7.06 for COAD; all p < 0.0001). No enrichment is observed in housekeeping or non-driver control gene sets, supporting signal specificity. An extended module enables structured evaluation of oncogenic potential, druggability, clinical relevance, and network vulnerability, supporting end-to-end interpretation from candidate identification to biological hypothesis generation. RegNetAgents establishes an interpretable AI framework for cross-network regulatory candidate identification in cancer genomics.

</details>


### [254] [DialogueVPR: Towards Conversational Visual Place Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14115)
*Yukun Song,Changwei Wang,Xingtian Pei,Shibiao Xu,Wenhao Xu,Shunpeng Chen,Yu Zhang,Ke Zhang,Rongtao Xu,Xuxiang Feng,Pengyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出对话式地点识别(DlgPR)范式，将语言引导的地理定位从静态单次检索转变为交互式对话推理过程，并构建了首个大规模对话基准DlgQuest-Cities。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于"语言引导的地理定位(language-guided geo-localization)"，属于多模态检索和视觉-语言导航领域，不涉及遥感卫星图像、目标检测、模型轻量化或星上部署等核心内容。虽然"geo-localization"与遥感有概念交集，但该工作面向的是街景/城市级别的视觉定位，而非遥感卫星图像中的目标检测任务。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：语言引导的地理定位(language-guided geo-localization)、对话式地点识别(dialogue-based place recognition)；应用场景：城市级视觉定位；传感器类型：摘要未说明（推测为街景图像）；目标类别：地点/位置识别，非目标检测。

Model architecture: 统一推理框架，包含：(1) 跨模态多级检索器(cross-modal multi-level retriever)；(2) 智能提问器DQ-pilot。DQ-pilot训练采用课程学习：先在DQ-cities-20k子集上进行监督微调(SFT)，再在更难的DQ-cities-10k子集上通过GRPO进行强化学习优化。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未涉及）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。该工作不涉及星上或边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：DlgQuest-Cities（首个大规模对话式地点识别基准），包含DQ-cities-20k（用于SFT）和DQ-cities-10k（用于强化学习）；评价指标：Discriminative Difficulty Index (DDI)用于课程采样，Positional Retrieval Gain (PRG)作为奖励函数直接衡量问题带来的检索提升。

Experiments: 实验表明基于推理的方法显著优于基线方法(baselines)。具体对比方法和消融实验细节摘要未说明。

Limitations: (1) 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；(2) 未涉及任何遥感图像、卫星数据或星上处理场景；(3) 未讨论模型效率、推理速度或部署可行性；(4) 方法复杂度较高（多级检索+对话推理），不适合资源受限的星上环境。

Ideas for my research: 借鉴意义非常有限。唯一可能的间接启发：(1) 课程学习(curriculum learning)和强化学习(GRPO)的训练策略可迁移到遥感检测模型的训练优化中；(2) 多级检索(multi-level retrieval)的设计思路或许可类比遥感检测中的多尺度特征融合。但整体而言，该论文与"在轨遥感目标检测模型轻量化"课题方向差异过大，不建议深入参考。

Abstract: Inspired by how humans communicate spatial information, language-guided geo-localization has gained significant traction for its intuitive and practical value. Despite this progress, most methods still rely on a static, one-shot retrieval paradigm, which fails to handle the ambiguity and incompleteness inherent in real-world natural language descriptions. We propose a paradigm shift to reasoning retrieval and introduce Dialogue Place Recognition (DlgPR), which casts localization as an interactive, dialogue-driven reasoning process. To support this new task, we present DlgQuest-Cities, the first large-scale dialogue-based benchmark for place recognition, and a unified reasoning framework that couples a cross-modal multi-level retriever with an intelligent questioner, DQ-pilot. DQ-pilot is trained in a curriculum: supervised fine-tuning on a curated DQ-cities-20k subset followed by reinforcement refinement on a harder DQ-cities-10k split via GRPO. Two task-aligned metrics guide learning: a Discriminative Difficulty Index (DDI) for curriculum sampling and a Positional Retrieval Gain (PRG) reward that directly measures retrieval improvement induced by a question. Experiments show this reasoning-based approach significantly outperforms baselines. The code and model are available at https://github.com/Graysonggg/DlgPR.

</details>


### [255] [Interpretable Language Model for Closed-Loop Type 1 Diabetes Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14126)
*Maya Sarkar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-T1D，利用知识蒸馏将强化学习策略迁移到大语言模型（LLaMA 3.1 8B/Qwen3 8B），用于1型糖尿病的胰岛素泵控制，实现可解释的血糖管理。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文研究的是1型糖尿病胰岛素泵控制，属于医疗健康领域，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱/多光谱、空间在轨智能等任何遥感相关主题，也不涉及目标检测或遥感场景理解。虽然使用了知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）这一轻量化相关技术，但应用场景和任务类型与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关。

Reading priority: 1分，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务：胰岛素泵控制（insulin pump control），血糖管理；场景：1型糖尿病治疗；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: 基于LLaMA 3.1 8B和Qwen3 8B的大语言模型，通过知识蒸馏从强化学习专家系统中学习。

Lightweight method: 知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation），将RL专家系统的知识迁移到LLM中。但蒸馏目标是8B参数级别的LLM，并非轻量化模型，参数量巨大，不适合边缘/星上部署。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。LLaMA 3.1 8B和Qwen3 8B均为80亿参数级别的大模型，推理资源需求极高，完全不适用于星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：FDA批准的UVA/Padova T1D模拟器（非遥感数据集）；评价指标：Time in Range（血糖控制时间百分比，73.5%），以及形式化安全验证（formal safety verification）。

Experiments: LLM控制器在血糖控制上超越了原始的RL专家系统（73.5% Time in Range），并具备可解释性和形式化安全验证能力。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 模型参数量巨大（8B级别），不具备轻量化部署可行性；3. 仅在医疗模拟器上验证，未涉及任何遥感数据或场景。

Ideas for my research: 可借鉴点极为有限。唯一可能相关的是知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）这一技术思路——将大模型（如检测模型）的知识蒸馏到轻量小模型中，但本文的蒸馏目标仍是8B大模型，与轻量化方向背道而驰。不建议作为主要参考。

Abstract: Type 1 Diabetes (T1D) is a chronic, life-threatening autoimmune condition characterized by the complete destruction of insulin-producing pancreatic beta cells. While Artificial Pancreas Systems (APS) powered by Reinforcement Learning (RL) have shown promise in automating insulin delivery, their ``black-box'' nature makes it hard for patients and doctors to trust them fully. This paper presents LLM-T1D, a promising approach that combines the precision of RL with the clear, human-like reasoning of Large Language Models (LLMs) to create a more transparent and reliable insulin pump controller. By training an expert RL system and distilling its knowledge into fine-tuned LLaMA 3.1 8B and Qwen3 8B models, we developed a controller that not only surpasses the RL system's performance but also explains its decisions in plain, understandable language. Tested on the FDA-approved UVA/Padova T1D simulator, the LLM controllers deliver excellent blood sugar control (73.5% Time in Range) while maintaining strict formal safety verification against hallucinations.

</details>


### [256] [Human AI Construction of Bayesian Networks for Operational Decision Support -- A Virtual Survey Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14141)
*Kumar Rahul,Shovan Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种利用大语言模型和AI智能体面板来构建贝叶斯信念网络的新方法，应用于医疗决策场景。

Research relevance: 1分。与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关。论文主题是贝叶斯信念网络的构建方法，应用场景是医疗决策（alternative healthcare），不涉及任何遥感图像、目标检测或模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：贝叶斯网络结构学习与参数估计；应用场景：医疗决策（患者就医意向建模）；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: BBN（贝叶斯信念网络），结合LLM（大语言模型）和AI智能体面板，使用trimmed-mean规则去噪。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。不涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集名称。使用医疗决策场景的客户意向数据（非公开遥感数据集）。

Experiments: 通过案例研究展示了模型在建模客户就医意向上的效果，发现自我效能（self efficacy）实际因果影响小，而主观规范（subjective norms）影响更强。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的遥感或轻量化技术内容。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴意义。该论文属于贝叶斯网络与LLM结合的概率建模方向，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Bayesian Belief Networks (BBNs) are powerful tools for decision-making under uncertainty. However, building their structures and estimating parameters are difficult. Currently, researchers must choose between relying on expert judgement or using large datasets to learn the structure and parameters of the network. We propose a new methodology using Large Language Models to bridge the gap between expert opinion and data-driven learning. This approach uses a panel of AI agents to estimate probabilities based on specific personas and context. We then apply a trimmed-mean rule to remove noise from these responses. We develop a six step BBN framework and illustrate it to model customer intention to consult a doctor in an alternative healthcare system. The model reveals that while self efficacy appears to be a major factor, its actual causal impact is small. In contrast, subjective norms have a much stronger effect in modelling customers' intention. The most effective strategy is to improve both confidence and community norms simultaneously.

</details>


### [257] [Capability from Access Structure, Not Scale: Lower Bounds and Pre-Registered Tests for Hybrid Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14144)
*Wenhui Chen,Jianlin Chen,Ziyao Lin,Chi Man Vong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出能力收敛假说(CCH)，认为在固定推理预算下，不同架构的表示会收敛但能力不会收敛，并论证需要混合通道架构(压缩通道+可扩展索引通道)才能跨越三个资源墙(Shannon墙、Horizon墙、Circuit墙)。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文是纯理论性的机器学习/信息论研究，讨论的是通用神经网络架构的表示收敛与能力收敛问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何遥感相关场景，也不涉及目标检测任务，更不涉及轻量化部署方法。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明。论文讨论的是通用神经网络架构理论，任务为Newton's-apple problem（一个理论性推理任务），不涉及任何遥感场景、传感器类型或目标类别。

Model architecture: 讨论的是通用架构类别：compressive O(1)-state channel（压缩通道）和 scalable verbatim-index channel（可扩展索引通道）的混合架构，以及attention-only、固定窗口等理论架构类型。不涉及具体的目标检测模型结构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文讨论的是理论上的资源墙（Shannon wall, horizon wall, circuit wall）和per-token推理预算约束，但未提出任何具体的轻量化方法（如剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集。实验为小规模预注册测试，指标包括exact-retrieval error（精确检索误差）、state-tracking bifurcation（状态跟踪分岔）等理论性指标。

Experiments: 报告了预注册的小规模测试：预测的剪刀差被测量到（64标量状态加上一层全局注意力后，精确检索误差从0.994降至0.000），状态跟踪分岔落在注册边界上，联合见证显示不可约的双通道解。有一个预测失败且方向反转。

Limitations: 论文本身是理论性工作，与遥感在轨目标检测完全无关。其理论框架（CCH）讨论的是通用神经网络的表示收敛与能力收敛，无法直接应用于遥感目标检测模型的轻量化或星上部署。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关。虽然论文不直接涉及遥感或目标检测，但其关于"固定推理预算下不同架构的能力收敛"的理论视角，可能对理解轻量化模型在资源受限（如星上）条件下的能力边界有间接启发意义。但整体借鉴价值很低。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) holds that as models scale, representations of heterogeneous networks converge toward a shared model of reality. We propose its sequel and boundary, the Capability Convergence Hypothesis (CCH): under a fixed per-token inference budget, representational convergence does not entail capability convergence. Capability instead converges toward a class, the access-complete hybrid: any architecture holding both a compressive O(1)-state channel and a scalable verbatim-index channel. We anchor it on a witness task, the Newton's-apple problem in an infinite stream, and name three resource walls: a Shannon wall barring any o(Nb)-state architecture, a horizon wall barring any fixed window, and a circuit wall barring fixed-depth attention-only composition (conditional on TC0 != NC1). Under an explicit separability assumption a hybrid crosses all three by paying each wall's price, so capability is strictly super-additive under composition. We separate what we prove from what we conjecture: the access-completeness principle rests on information-theoretic lower bounds and pre-registered experiments, while the field-level convergence trend is an economics-motivated conjecture. We report the first pre-registered small-scale tests under criteria frozen before the data: the predicted scissors gap is measured (exact-retrieval error 0.994 vs. 0.000 once a 64-scalar state gains one global-attention layer), the state-tracking bifurcation lands at the registered boundary, and a conjunction witness shows an irreducibly two-channel solution; one prediction failed with its direction reversed and is reported as such. Representational convergence is given freely by scale; capability convergence must be purchased by access structure.

</details>


### [258] [ToolAnchor: Anchoring Counterfactual Context to Boost Agentic Tool-use Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14145)
*Weiting Liu,Jieyi Bi,Wanqi Zhou,Jianfeng Feng,Yining Ma,Ai Han,Wenlian Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ToolAnchor框架，通过反事实锚定上下文打破大语言模型智能体在工具集扩展时的行为惯性，实现动态工具适应。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于大语言模型智能体（LLM agent）的工具扩展与动态适应研究，与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"方向基本无关。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间精读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型智能体的工具集扩展与动态适应；应用场景：通用AI助手（GAIA）、文本搜索（BrowseComp）、视觉搜索（VDR-Bench）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: ToolAnchor框架，包含teacher模型生成反事实上下文、student模型rollout验证、agentic post-training内化成功干预。基于大语言模型智能体架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。论文未讨论星上/边缘部署、参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: GAIA（通用AI助手任务）、BrowseComp（文本搜索）、VDR-Bench（视觉搜索）。评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: 在GAIA、BrowseComp、VDR-Bench三个基准上评估，ToolAnchor在扩展工具集下表现出competitive performance。摘要未提供具体数值对比或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测完全无关；2. 未讨论计算效率、模型大小等部署约束；3. 仅在通用NLP/视觉搜索任务上验证，未涉及遥感场景；4. 未提供任何轻量化或边缘部署相关信息。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。但"行为惯性"概念在模型压缩/剪枝场景中可能有类比意义：当剪枝后模型需要适应新硬件或新任务时，也可能存在"回退到原始行为模式"的问题。不过该论文的方法论（反事实上下文注入+agentic post-training）与遥感目标检测轻量化差距较大，不建议直接借鉴。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model agents excel at long-horizon tasks, yet they are typically post-trained on fixed toolsets. When tasks demand new tools, these agents struggle to incorporate them effectively, and retraining from scratch is often impractical. We identify the core obstacle in such toolset expansion problem as behavioral inertia: the tendency of agents to fall back on familiar tools and established reasoning patterns despite having access to new ones. We demonstrate that injecting counterfactual anchor contexts at critical decision points can break this inertia, recovering failed trajectories by eliciting suppressed agent capabilities. To scale this insight, we propose ToolAnchor, a framework that uses teacher models to hypothesize these counterfactual contexts, verifies them via student rollouts, and internalizes the successful interventions through agentic post-training. Extensive evaluations across general AI assistant (GAIA), textual search (BrowseComp), and visual search (VDR-Bench) tasks demonstrate that ToolAnchor consistently exhibits competitive performance under expanded toolsets. Our work bridges the gap between static post-training and dynamic adaptation, charting a new path for scalable agentic reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [259] [Enhancing Small Language Models Reasoning through Knowledge Graph Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14149)
*Dimitrios Kelesis,Konstantinos Bougiatiotis,Georgios Paliouras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种神经符号代理框架，利用工具调用（extract_facts和get_hint）增强小语言模型在多跳逻辑推理任务上的能力，在CLUTRR基准上测试Gemma 3和Llama 3.2。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于小语言模型（SLM）的符号推理增强，不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化部署等任何相关主题。

Reading priority: 1分。该论文与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：多跳逻辑推理（kinship reasoning）；应用场景：自然语言推理；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。摘要未说明遥感相关场景。

Model architecture: 神经符号代理框架：SLM（Gemma 3 1B/4B, Llama 3.2 3B）作为agent，调用两个工具——extract_facts（符号三元组提取）和get_hint（通过RGCN进行专家推理）。摘要未说明轻量化网络设计。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS等任何模型压缩或加速方法。虽然使用了小模型（1B-4B参数），但这是模型选择而非轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等任何部署相关信息。不涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：CLUTRR（kinship benchmark）；评价指标：摘要未明确说明具体指标，提及"performance gain 1.5-2x over story-only baselines"。

Experiments: 两种配置：Oracle场景（使用真实三元组）和Realistic场景（依赖自提取知识）。结果显示RGCN提示相比仅故事基线有1.5-2倍性能提升，但受限于提取瓶颈和顺序推理脆弱性。存在"distraction effect"——噪声自生成事实会降低性能。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化完全无关；2. 提取瓶颈（extraction bottleneck）导致早期错误在多跳链中累积；3. 特定架构中存在"distraction effect"；4. 未涉及任何视觉/遥感图像处理。

Ideas for my research: 该论文与课题方向基本无关，无直接可借鉴点。但神经符号方法中"工具调用"的设计思想（将复杂任务分解为原子操作）在理论上可类比于遥感目标检测中的多阶段pipeline设计，不过关联性极弱。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) have set benchmarks for zero-shot reasoning, their deployment remains cost-prohibitive and environmentally taxing. Small Language Models (SLMs) offer a sustainable alternative, but prone to errors, on tasks requiring complex, multi-hop logical grounding. We investigate a neuro-symbolic agentic framework to enhance the reasoning capabilities of SLMs, specifically Gemma 3 (1B, 4B) and Llama 3.2 (3B), using the CLUTRR kinship benchmark. Our approach transforms the SLM into a minimalist agent utilizing two specialized tool calls: extract_facts for symbolic triplet extraction and get_hint for expert reasoning via a Relational Graph Convolutional Network (RGCN). We evaluate these models across two configurations, both in an Oracle scenario with ground-truth triplets and a Realistic scenario relying on self-extracted knowledge. Our results reveal that while RGCN-derived hints provide a 1.5 - 2x performance gain over story-only baselines, the system is constrained by the extraction bottleneck and sequential deductive fragility, where early extraction errors compound over multi-hop chains. Furthermore, we identify a "distraction effect" in specific architectures where noisy, self-generated facts degrade performance despite the presence of expert hints. This work characterizes the challenges of symbolic grounding in low-resource agentic systems and provides a roadmap for iterative verification in neuro-symbolic agentic pipelines.

</details>

Abstract: This position paper explores how Agentic AI and Model Context Protocol (MCP) can support power-grid studies in a Transmission System Operator (TSO) context. We focus on integrating Large Language Models with numerical simulation tools, structured workflows, and human supervision. We identify key industrial requirements for agent assisted grid studies and introduce pypowsybl-mcp, an MCP-based interface exposing selected capabilities of our simulation tool, pypowsybl to AI agents. This first step provides a testbed to study how agents can setup simulations, execute analyses, retrieve results, and interact with power-system simulators through standardized tool calls. We also discuss principles for human-in-the-loop, multi-agent workflows and outline an evaluation strategy combining technical metrics and practitioner feedback. The paper positions MCP-based tool integration as a step toward more interactive, auditable, and scalable grid-study environments.

</details>


### [261] [MemoHarness: Agent Harnesses That Learn from Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14159)
*Yue Huang,Wenjie Wang,Han Bao,Yuchen Ma,Xiaonan Luo,Yi Nian,Haomin Zhuang,Zheyuan Liu,Yue Zhao,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MemoHarness，一种通过执行经验自适应优化LLM Agent控制层（harness）的框架，将harness分解为六个可编辑维度，利用双层经验库存储诊断信息和全局模式，实现无需测试标签的自适应推理。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化方向基本无关。论文聚焦于LLM Agent的harness（外部控制层）自适应优化，属于自然语言处理/Agent系统领域，不涉及遥感图像、目标检测、模型压缩或边缘部署等核心主题。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM Agent harness自适应优化；应用场景：shell-agent、代码生成、分析推理；传感器类型：不适用；目标类别：不适用。

Model architecture: MemoHarness框架：将harness分解为context、tools、orchestration、memory、decoding、output handling六个可编辑控制维度；双层经验库（per-case diagnoses + distilled global patterns）；基于检索的经验自适应机制。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。论文未讨论任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文讨论了检索经验的缓存可降低额外上下文开销（cost-competitive），但未涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署指标，更未涉及星上/边缘部署。

Datasets and metrics: 评估基准：shell-agent、code-generation、analytical-reasoning benchmarks；评价指标：摘要未说明具体指标名称。

Experiments: MemoHarness相比固定harness有改进，展示了对未见任务套件和基座模型的选择性迁移能力。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 论文自身局限性：统计鲁棒性和组件归因分析留作未来工作；3. 对在轨遥感目标检测轻量化课题无直接借鉴价值。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文属于LLM Agent系统领域，与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向无交集。

Abstract: An agent harness is the external control layer that turns a base LLM into an executable agent by managing context, tools, orchestration, memory, decoding, and output handling. While harness design strongly affects agent behavior, most automatic improvement methods optimize narrower artifacts such as prompts, pipelines, or workflows, and deployed agents usually reuse a single global harness for all cases. We introduce MemoHarness, an adaptive harness optimization framework that learns from its own executions. MemoHarness decomposes the harness into six editable control dimensions, stores per-case diagnoses and distilled global patterns in a dual-layer experience bank, and adapts the learned harness to each test case using retrieved experience without test-time labels, feedback, or additional search. In our evaluation across shell-agent, code-generation, and analytical-reasoning benchmarks, MemoHarness improves over the fixed harnesses we compare against and shows selective transfer to unseen suites and base models. Its additional context can also remain cost-competitive when much of the retrieved experience is cacheable. These results provide evidence that execution experience is a practical substrate for building agent harnesses that are more adaptive than a single static configuration, while leaving broader claims about statistical robustness and component attribution to future work.

</details>


### [262] [When a Verified World Model Still Loses: Play-Adequacy vs Prediction-Accuracy in LLM-Synthesized Code World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14169)
*Javier Aguilar Martín*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了LLM合成的代码世界模型（CWM）即使通过高精度采样验证，仍可能在规划中系统性失败，因为其遗漏的关键动态规则恰好是规划搜索分布中的关键部分，并提出了"验证-正确差距"的概念。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文研究的是LLM合成游戏规则的世界模型及其在经典规划中的可靠性问题，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测与模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议投入时间阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：LLM合成代码世界模型（CWM）用于游戏规则建模与规划；应用场景：游戏（如Atari类游戏、Kuhn扑克）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: LLM（GPT系列）用于从自然语言描述中合成可执行代码形式的游戏规则模型，结合经典规划器进行搜索。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何轻量化方法。摘要未提及剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等模型压缩或加速技术。

Onboard deployability: 完全不涉及星上/边缘部署。摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 使用游戏环境（如Atari类游戏、Kuhn扑克）进行实验；评价指标包括：transition accuracy（转移准确率）、state accuracy（状态准确率）、play cost（游戏代价）、seed-clustered 95% CI；n=4800次游戏测试。

Experiments: 主要发现：(1) CWM在采样轨迹上可达100%转移准确率、≥98%状态准确率，但游戏中系统性失败；(2) 提出danger = play_cost × (1 - rarity)^N的定量规律；(3) 增加数据（包括DAgger和针对性样本）无法修复该问题；(4) 在不完美信息CWM中证明了覆盖界（size-N gate identifying when N ≳ b^{d_max}），并构造了Beacon反例。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法直接借鉴。该论文聚焦于LLM合成世界模型在规划中的验证可靠性问题，属于AI规划与LLM推理领域。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文讨论的是LLM合成世界模型的验证-正确差距问题，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题在问题定义、技术路线、评估体系上均无交集。

Abstract: Large language models can synthesize a game's rules as executable code - a Code World Model (CWM) - which a classical planner then searches over. Such models are typically accepted when they reach high transition accuracy on sampled trajectories. We argue this is the wrong notion of adequacy for planning.
  We show four things. (1) An LLM-synthesized CWM can pass a sampling gate at 100% transition accuracy and be $\geq 98\%$ state-accurate on the planner's own search distribution, yet lose systematically at play, because the $<1\%$ it gets wrong is exactly the pivotal dynamics; the play cost of the omitted rule is $0.091$ (seed-clustered 95% CI $[0.065,0.117]$, $n=4800$). We call this the verified-vs-correct gap, and confirm it end-to-end through the synthesis pipeline. (2) The harm follows a quantitative law, $\mathrm{danger}=\mathrm{play\_cost}\times(1-\mathrm{rarity})^N$, whose $(1-\mathrm{rarity})^N$ gate-miss factor is proven exact and whose play cost is empirically bounded. (3) The failure is not repaired by more data: LLM synthesis behaves as rule translation, not rule inference, and did not infer the omitted rule across models (GPT-5.x) and data regimes (including DAgger and targeted examples). (4) The same mechanism recurs on the belief-inference function of imperfect-information CWMs: we prove a coverage bound (a size-$N$ gate is identifying when $N\gtrsim b^{d_{\max}}$), explaining why shallow games such as Kuhn poker show no gap, and hand-construct Beacon, a verified-but-wrong inference function that passes the gate yet loses every game.
  These results suggest adequacy for planning-oriented world models should be measured on the search distribution or by play directly, not by prediction accuracy on sampled transitions.

</details>


### [263] [ReasFlow: Assisting Reasoning-Centric Scientific Discovery in Applied Mathematics via a Knowledge-Based Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14178)
*Yutong He,Daibo Li,Guohong Li,Jiahe Geng,Zhengyang Huang,Can Ren,Zekun Zhang,Yifan Liu,Shuchen Zhu,Hengrui Zhang,Boao Kong,Ming Sun,Shu Li,Chenyi Li,Jiang Hu,Kun Yuan,Zaiwen Wen,Pingwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个面向理论驱动型科学发现的端到端自主智能体系统ReasFlow，用于自动生成包含严格理论推导和实验验证的研究论文。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感、卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化完全无关，属于AI for Science/大语言模型智能体方向，不涉及任何遥感图像处理、目标检测或模型压缩技术。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：科学发现自主智能体；应用场景：理论驱动型学科（数学、理论物理等）；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: ReasFlow系统包含：(i) 内部验证循环（internal verification loop），用于审计逻辑一致性；(ii) 自动知识检索与自我改进机制（automated knowledge retrieval and self-improvement mechanism）。整体为LLM驱动的多智能体协作架构。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。摘要未提及任何剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS或轻量网络设计。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。摘要未提及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗或实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 评估方式：基于LLM的评审标准（LLM-based review rubric），对比SOTA开源基线。摘要未说明具体数据集。

Experiments: 系统自主生成了5篇完整研究论文，在LLM评审标准下取得最高分。摘要未提供定量对比结果。

Limitations: 与遥感在轨目标检测轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术细节。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于LLM智能体用于科学发现，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题无任何交集。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models have fueled autonomous AI agents capable of tackling complex scientific tasks, yet existing automated research systems remain predominantly focused on empirically driven domains with quantitative benchmarks, leaving theory-driven discovery, particularly in mathematically grounded disciplines requiring rigorous proofs and synthesis of domain knowledge, largely underexplored. Key challenges include the difficulty of verifying theoretical reasoning at scale, insufficient reasoning ability for autonomous frontier exploration, and a scarcity of procedural heuristics in the literature. We introduce ReasFlow, an end-to-end autonomous agent system for reasoning-centric scientific discovery that operationalizes a collaborative paradigm where the human expert acts as Principal Investigator while the agent executes rigorous derivations as a capable graduate student. ReasFlow incorporates (i) a robust internal verification loop that audits logical coherence and corrects fundamental errors prior to human inspection, and (ii) an automated knowledge retrieval and self-improvement mechanism that proactively surfaces both declarative facts and overlooked procedural heuristics, substantially reducing expert intervention. The system unifies literature synthesis, algorithm design, theorem proving, experimentation, and manuscript preparation in a single system. Deployed to autonomously generate five complete research papers with rigorous theoretical and empirical content from minimal prompts, ReasFlow consistently achieves the highest evaluation scores among state-of-the-art open-access baselines under a curated LLM-based review rubric. ReasFlow is publicly accessible via the ReasLab platform, providing a collaborative workspace for AI-assisted theoretical research. Github repo: https://github.com/ReasLab/ReasFlow.git.

</details>


### [264] [RxBrain: Embodied Cognition Foundation Model with Joint Language-Visual Reasoning and Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14187)
*Haotian Liang,Mingkang Chen,Yufei Huang,Yuchun Guo,Xiaomeng Zhu,Xiangli Shi,Kaixuan Wang,Yunxuan Mao,Weijie Zhou,Ling Chen,Shirong Zeng,Yueyu Long,Yuchen Si,Yajuan Zhu,Xingyu Zhou,Minghui Wang,Wanjia He,Xin Yang,Lingzhu Xiang,Zhiqing Liu,Bohan Ma,Xiran Huang,Tianshuo Yang,Zhiheng Liu,Xuantang Xiong,Zisheng Lu,Ping Luo,Yao Mu,Han Hu,Zhengyou Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个面向具身认知的多模态基础模型RxBrain，通过联合语言-视觉推理与想象实现任务规划，采用统一的Mixture-of-Transformers架构同时支持语言、图像、视频的理解与生成。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。该论文聚焦于具身认知(embodied cognition)、机器人任务规划(robot action planning)、多模态基础模型，完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化。没有任何遥感场景、卫星在轨部署或目标检测相关内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与"遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化"研究方向基本无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：具身认知任务规划、机器人动作生成；应用场景：机器人操作、具身智能；传感器类型：RGB视觉（从embodied videos中提取）；目标类别：无目标检测相关类别。摘要未说明遥感、卫星、aerial image、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等任何与遥感相关的内容。

Model architecture: 统一多模态Mixture-of-Transformers架构，支持语言、图像、视频的理解与生成。摘要未说明具体参数量、层数等细节。

Lightweight method: 摘要未涉及任何轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等任何部署相关信息。该模型面向机器人具身智能，非星上/边缘部署场景。

Datasets and metrics: 自建RxBrain-Bench评估基准，用于评测模型是否能够通过联合文本-视觉组件表示具身规划。数据来自embodied videos自动转换的文本-视觉规划监督。摘要未说明具体数据集规模、指标名称。

Experiments: 实验表明RxBrain保持了具身理解和生成能力，能生成耦合文本推理、世界状态预测和联合子目标规划的规划序列。在连续机器人动作生成任务上展示了有前景的真实机器人性能，无需大规模动作数据预训练。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无法提供任何直接借鉴。该论文面向具身认知和机器人规划，属于完全不同的研究领域。

Ideas for my research: 无直接可借鉴点。该论文与遥感卫星在轨目标检测模型轻量化课题的研究方向、任务目标、应用场景均不相关。

Abstract: Embodied cognition requires agents to connect high-level task reasoning with the physical states to be achieved. We introduce Hy-Embodied-RxBrain, an embodied cognition foundation model with joint language-visual reasoning and imagination. Unlike vision-language models that emphasize scene understanding and textual decision making, or generative world models that mainly predict future visual states, RxBrain represents embodied plans in a single planning sequence where language and visual imagination play complementary roles. Language provides the abstract structure of a plan, including task decomposition, planning primitives, constraints, temporal order, and decision logic, while visual imagination grounds this structure through world state prediction and joint subgoal planning, associating each planning step with intermediate and final physical states. RxBrain adopts a unified multimodal Mixture-of-Transformers architecture that supports language, image, and video understanding and generation within one model. To train this capability, we build an automatic pipeline that converts embodied videos into joint text-visual planning supervision by decomposing videos into planning steps and aligning them with visual state transitions. We further introduce RxBrain-Bench to evaluate whether models can represent embodied plans through joint textual and visual components rather than separate understanding or generation. Experiments show that RxBrain maintains embodied understanding and generation abilities, and produces plans with coupled textual reasoning, world state prediction, and joint subgoal planning. We also extend RxBrain to continuous robot action generation, where it shows promising real-robot performance without large-scale action-data pretraining. These results provide an initial step toward foundation models for embodied cognition.

</details>


### [265] [How Artificial Intelligence LLM Engines Shape the Global Conflict Information Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14197)
*Jason Miklian*

Main category: cs.AI

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 任务类型：大语言模型问答准确性评估与幻觉模式分析；应用场景：冲突信息环境、信息战、GEO；传感器类型：不涉及；目标类别：不涉及。

Model architecture: 不涉及目标检测或遥感模型架构。论文评估了5种AI answer engines（大语言模型驱动的问答引擎），但未说明具体模型架构名称。

Lightweight method: 不涉及任何模型轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS、轻量网络等）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及星上/边缘部署。无参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性等部署相关信息。

Datasets and metrics: 数据集：针对28个冲突的问答数据集，共5,460个答案；评价指标：答案与文献证据的一致性评分（幻觉率、错误归因率、错误计数率）；另分析了1,048个被LLM引用的冲突事实网站。

Experiments: 主要发现：冲突相关可检索记录越少，LLM幻觉越严重；薄记录更容易被GEO操纵以偏置引擎响应；国家党派数字捕获虽处于初期但增长迅速。

Limitations: 与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及轻量化方向完全无关，无任何可借鉴的技术方法或实验设置。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文属于NLP/社会科学交叉领域，不涉及计算机视觉、遥感图像处理、模型压缩或边缘部署等任何与课题相关的技术内容。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) answer engines now field a growing share of the questions that analysts, scholars, and the public ask about issues of peace and conflict. Large Language Models (LLMs) are known to hallucinate under certain conditions, but do these errors have discernible patterns when they are asked about conflicts, and if so what can that teach us about the changing global conflict information environment? To answer, we first asked a battery of questions about 28 conflicts to five leading answer engines and scored their 5,460 answers against documented evidence. We found that the thinner the retrievable record around a given conflict, the more the engines invent, misattribute, and miscount. Thin records don't just encourage hallucination, but create structural exposure to mis- and disinformation, because they are the easiest records to warp through Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) to bias engine responses. Through an analysis of 1,048 websites that the AI LLMs pulled conflict facts from, we found that GEO source optimization is already happening, and while state-partisan digital capture remains incipient it is rapidly growing. We explain what these findings mean for scholarship with the rise of GEO information warfare, and for policy argue for a return to the deep local monitoring and translation-based research that AI tools cannot replicate, closing with a discussion of future research opportunities and challenges in this fast-moving space.

</details>


### [266] [Align AI to Dynamic Human-AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14240)
*Valerie Chen,Cleotilde Gonzalez,Anita Williams Woolley,Michael Lee,Tongshuang Wu,Vincent Conitzer,Aarti Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文主张将AI对齐从静态模仿人类偏好转向交互式、互补的对齐范式，强调偏好通过交互动态涌现，而非静态满足。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测或模型轻量化，属于AI对齐（AI alignment）与人类-人工智能交互的理论研究，与本研究方向基本无关。

Model architecture: 摘要未说明。论文未提出任何具体的模型架构。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及任何轻量化方法。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。论文不涉及部署相关指标。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明。论文未使用任何遥感或目标检测数据集。

Experiments: 摘要未说明。论文未报告任何实验。

Limitations: 该论文是纯理论/观点性文章，缺乏实验验证和具体技术方案，与遥感卫星在轨目标检测轻量化方向无任何交集。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。该论文讨论的是AI对齐的社会科学视角，与遥感在轨目标检测模型轻量化的技术问题完全无关。

Abstract: Current alignment approaches typically focus on emulating human behavior using static representations of human preferences, failing to capture the dynamic, context-dependent nature of real-world human-AI interactions. In this paper, we argue for a shift from static and emulative to interactive and complementary alignment, where preferences emerge through interaction and alignment is defined not by satisfying preferences alone. We first formalize this gap by contrasting existing alignment with a trajectory-level view in which human and model behavior co-evolve over time. Because these interaction dynamics have not been adequately captured within existing ML formulations, we ground this perspective in insights from an interdisciplinary workshop. We draw on lessons from social-science accounts of human-human collaboration and then argue that human-AI systems amplify these dynamics, introducing new asymmetries that make reasoning about uncertainty harder and introduce new coordination challenges. Based on these lessons and new challenges, we conclude by outlining a research agenda for developing AI systems that align with humans in interaction, requiring an interdisciplinary synthesis of machine learning and the social and decision sciences.

</details>


### [267] [The Steering Budget: Examples beat Knobs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14246)
*Raj Kumar Rajendran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究生成模型（图像/晶体结构生成）中通过"示例"而非"旋钮"（提示/引导尺度）来控制生成属性的方法，提出训练数据存在属性移动预算，示例可以覆盖旋钮无法触及的更大范围。

Research relevance: 1分——与本研究方向基本无关。论文完全不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、目标检测或模型轻量化，而是讨论生成模型（image generation和crystal-structure generation）中属性控制的方法论问题。

Reading priority: 1分——完全不相关。不涉及遥感、目标检测、轻量化或星上部署中的任何一个核心方向，无需阅读。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Model architecture: 摘要未说明具体模型架构。提及generative models，但未指定是GAN、Diffusion、VAE还是其他类型。

Lightweight method: 不涉及。本文未讨论任何模型压缩、加速或轻量化方法（剪枝、量化、蒸馏、NAS等均未提及）。

Onboard deployability: 不涉及。未讨论参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗、实时性或星上/边缘部署相关内容。

Datasets and metrics: 摘要未说明具体数据集和评价指标。仅在两个领域（image generation和crystal-structure generation）进行了实验验证。

Experiments: 摘要未说明具体实验结果和对比数据。仅提到在图像生成和晶体结构生成两个不相关领域验证了可证伪的声明。

Ideas for my research: 无借鉴点。本文讨论的是生成模型的属性控制机制（knob vs. example），与遥感在轨目标检测及模型轻量化没有任何技术交集。

Abstract: Generative models are steered with knobs -- prompts, guidance scales, property tags. Turn one as hard as you like and, past a point, it stops moving the property you care about. We find that ceiling is not a shortcoming of the model but a budget, set by the training data before the model is trained: a property's movable range splits in two -- the part a knob can reach, and a second, significant part that only examples -- concrete instances of what you want more of -- can reach. That second part is usually much larger, but not always, and the same budget says so in advance.
  Reaching that second part takes a different move: instead of turning a knob, you show the model examples, composed from what it already learned rather than added to its training. A cheap audit of the training data measures the budget; we give a recipe for building the example set that reaches all of it.
  This buys two things a knob can't. Reach: it moves a property across the whole budget, not just the part a knob reaches. Expressiveness: it steers toward targets you can only specify by example -- including ones you can't put into words. We turn these into a handful of falsifiable claims and verify them in two unrelated domains, image and crystal-structure generation -- marking where a knob is enough, and where only examples will do.

</details>


### [268] [Automatic Hard Example Synthesis with Multi-Level Agentic Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14256)
*Genglin Liu,Muye Zhang,Krishnamurthy Viswanathan,Nichole J. Hansen,Blaž Bratanič,Nathan L Clement,Shalini Ghosh,Ariel Fuxman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于多智能体架构的自动化红队测试框架，通过迭代生成对抗性多模态示例来提升大语言模型的内容安全鲁棒性。

Research relevance: 1分。该论文与遥感卫星、目标检测、模型轻量化三个方向均无任何关联。论文聚焦于多模态大语言模型(MLLM)的内容安全与对抗鲁棒性，属于AI安全与红队测试领域。

Reading priority: 1分。与遥感卫星在轨目标检测及模型轻量化研究方向基本无关，不建议投入阅读时间。

Model architecture: 多智能体架构：包含高推理能力的Architect agent（假设生成）、高级图像生成器、多级LLM评分委员会。使用test-time Retrieval将合成的对抗样本作为in-context demonstrations注入目标模型。

Datasets and metrics: 使用公开图像安全基准(public image safety benchmark)；评价指标：False Negative Rate (FNR)，从41.2%降至24.5%。

Experiments: 在公开图像安全基准上，FNR从41.2%降至24.5%，无需人工标注。摘要未提供更多对比实验或消融实验细节。

Limitations: 1. 与遥感/卫星/目标检测/轻量化完全无关；2. 仅报告FNR指标，未报告其他安全指标或计算开销；3. 未讨论对抗样本的可迁移性及对真实世界部署的影响。

Ideas for my research: 弱相关，借鉴价值有限。唯一可能的间接启发是：多智能体协作框架的思路或许可迁移到遥感数据自动标注或难例挖掘场景，但与本课题核心方向（在轨目标检测模型轻量化）关联度极低。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed for nuanced content safety and moderation tasks, yet they remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks and out-of-distribution edge cases. Traditional active learning and manual annotation fail to scale against the complexity and volume of novel multimodal threats. In this paper, we propose an automated, agentic red-teaming framework that systematically synthesizes difficult examples using an iterative strategy that proposes novel hypotheses as well as mutating on past attempts. Leveraging a multi-agent architecture that consists of a high-reasoning Architect agent, an advanced image generator, and a multi-level verification committee of LLM raters, our system autonomously uncovers boundary-pushing violations and ambiguous policy edge cases without any human intervention. By employing these carefully synthesized adversarial examples as in-context demonstrations via test-time Retrieval, we substantially improve the target model's robustness, reducing the False Negative Rate (FNR) from 41.2% to 24.5% in a public image safety benchmark without relying on any human labeling.

</details>


### [269] [AI Agents Do Not Fail Alone:The Context Fails First](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14275)
*Fouad Bousetouane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种衡量AI Agent上下文工程质量的评估框架ProofAgent-Harness，通过七项标准预测Agent行为可靠性，与遥感、目标检测、轻量化完全无关。

Research relevance: 1分，与本研究方向基本无关。论文聚焦于AI Agent的上下文工程（context engineering）质量评估，不涉及遥感、卫星、目标检测、轻量化或任何与在轨部署相关的内容。

Reading priority: 1分。与研究方向完全无关，不建议阅读。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Task and scene: 摘要未说明遥感场景。任务为AI Agent可靠性评估，场景为通用AI Agent应用（regulated agent domains），不涉及遥感、卫星、Earth observation、SAR、高光谱、多光谱等。

Lightweight method: 摘要未说明任何轻量化方法。不涉及剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏、NAS、实时推理、边缘部署等。

Onboard deployability: 摘要未说明。不涉及参数量、FLOPs、延迟、显存、能耗等部署相关信息。
